# Component Library Implementation Roadmap

Lifecycle: **Active**. Owner: Gabriel Bourget. Reviewed: **2026-10-19**. Codon UI owns the reusable React and React Native component library; Wavemap owns its later production mobile application. Native catalogue authority is the pinned React manifest at `ddc9f066f67af4742e05e04bd870726141c44129`: 74 registry items, including 53 components. Forty-three native counterparts have source implementation; that count does not certify complete installed or web-equivalent acceptance.

<a id="delivery-goal"></a>

## 3. Delivery Goal

Owner complexity direction on2026-10-23: native components should remain comparable in complexity to their canonical React components unless a concrete native requirement justifies more. Compare the actual reachable component behavior, including what its dependencies supply, rather than wrapper line counts alone. Prefer existing component primitives and scoped platform adapters; additional infrastructure must address a demonstrated component requirement. The unfinished field-input SDK prototype remains preserved while its approach is reassessed. This direction does not remove accepted capabilities, change the53-ID/477-cell finish line or count private construction as public delivery.

Status: **Active: complete M2 against the acceptance rubric and matrices below for all 53 catalogue IDs.** On 2026-10-17 Gabriel rejects the two-component finish line and requires completion conditions tied directly to precise acceptance rubrics and matrices. LinearProgress/Meter is a completed source checkpoint, not completion of this goal. Source count started at 18/53 and is now 43/53; the remaining 10 components and unresolved mandatory cells on existing components remain in scope. No component, batch, commit count, test count or wall-clock interval ends this goal.

Execute in dependency order on `feature/<description>` branches under §9.1, committing coherent contract/evidence, component/test and consumer/reference steps. Before each family, read its complete canonical source, inherited types/behavior and suites, then instantiate the exact capability/scenario rows and expected outcomes. Preserve controlled values, existing pins/palette, package ownership, sealed evidence, normal hooks and local Git identity. Use the established shared TypeScript/native style/state/ref/overlay patterns. Consolidate novel API/architecture/dependency/theme/registry/tooling decisions for owner review and continue unaffected rows while those decisions are pending. Branch pushes and library PR creation are authorized under §9.1. Merge, publication, workflow/distribution modifications, sibling edits and iPhone signing remain unauthorized.

The finish line is every applicable acceptance cell in sections [3.1.1–3.1.9](#31-acceptance-rubric), including the installed Android/iOS matrices. Stable `CAT.<manifest-id>.G01`–`G09` keys identify evidence cells only. The [evidence matrix](experiments.md#3-catalogue-acceptance-matrix) records the individual cells and proof links. `OPEN` means not yet reconciled or executed, not a failed test; `FAIL` and `BLOCKED` remain unresolved. A source/mock pass cannot close an installed cell. An agent-authored exclusion, a merely passing repeat or a diagnostic receipt cannot close a required cell. `N/A` needs the canonical-contract reason; `OWNER-DEFERRED` needs the exact existing/new owner authority and remains visibly deferred rather than passing. Existing physical/ARM/owner-visual deferrals are retained, not widened to emulator/simulator acceptance.

RN-TEXT-01 and original ComboBox failures keep their identities. They stop dependent installed/query propagation, not independent catalogue construction. Do not conduct further XCTest/LLDB/startup investigations while independent mandatory construction work remains. A later lab repair requires a materially new hypothesis and one goal-wide bounded attempt; repeated unchanged timeout investigations do not restart the workstream. If a required cell is externally blocked, preserve it, move to another incomplete cell and keep the goal incomplete. Exhausting all independently actionable work produces an explicit blocking decision, not a claim of completion.

<a id="acceptance-rubric"></a>

### 3.1. Acceptance Rubric

These rows operationalize the canonical web assertion baseline, existing native contracts, historical ledger Section 7.1–7.3 at `db54a8a`, Select's approved contract/installed matrix and searchable-selection priority gate. Their historical source paths and revisions remain in [the lookup](experiments.md#12-historical-document-lookup). They do not silently reapprove a deferred web capability. A new family instantiates row IDs before implementation; the ledger identifies the exact assertion/scenario and its verifier, revision, expected output and observed result.

<a id="canonical-capability-and-contract"></a>

#### 3.1.1. Canonical Capability And Contract

Evidence key: `G01`.

**Pass condition:** Enumerate every owned and inherited prop/default, state axis, callback/order/veto, ref/event, slot, geometry/size/color/order, keyboard/collection/formatting behavior and every assertion in the complete canonical web suites. Give each a native equivalent or an explicit owner-approved exclusion. No ignored prop or unreviewed exception.

**Closing evidence:** `CAP.<manifest-id>.<n>` rows naming canonical symbol/assertion, native contract/call site and expected outcome; approved decision reference for deviations. Every capability row must be dispositioned; merely linking a root suite is insufficient.

<a id="ownership-and-behavior"></a>

#### 3.1.2. Ownership And Behavior

Evidence key: `G02`.

**Pass condition:** Apply scenarios 3.2.1–3.2.6 to every supported state axis and action. Explicit false/0/empty string/empty array/contract-defined null remain distinct from undefined. Controlled requests wait for owner echo; repeated owner vetoes never mutate authoritative output. Defaults initialize once, callback order/count/guards remain correct, and removal/reinsertion/teardown produces no fabricated change.

**Closing evidence:** Named public-outcome tests with positive/negative requests, controlled/default transitions and exact callback traces. Add installed evidence wherever the observable behavior depends on native input, presentation, keyboard or gesture delivery.

<a id="executable-public-boundary"></a>

#### 3.1.3. Executable Public Boundary

Evidence key: `G03`.

**Pass condition:** Export only implemented props/types/constants; preserve native refs, identity/name precedence and original allowed event payloads. Require/validate names under the accepted contract, consume wrapper props and prevent aliases/child props from replacing owned values or handlers. Unsupported DOM/ownership surfaces fail executable public types.

**Closing evidence:** Positive/negative public call sites, native contract typecheck and compiled declarations, ref/native-event/host-prop assertions. Tests must actually fail for rejected cases rather than contain unused error directives.

<a id="customization-and-theme"></a>

#### 3.1.4. Customization And Theme

Evidence key: `G04`.

**Pass condition:** Cover every canonical geometry, size, order, mode, state and named style/content/icon slot. Both theme modes and mounted provider changes preserve current values. Computed → customStyles → final style and post-custom callback defaultStyle hold independently per owner; native dynamic colors and registered/array styles work and unsupported colors reject. Scaling stays enabled unless explicitly constrained.

**Closing evidence:** `STYLE.<manifest-id>.<n>` assertion mapping for every canonical customization assertion, native slot/state assertions and affected installed scenario 3.2.7–3.2.10 captures/measurements. No source/pixel equivalence inferred from object equality.

<a id="source-and-composition"></a>

#### 3.1.5. Source And Composition

Evidence key: `G05`.

**Pass condition:** Focused source and maintained-scene tests exercise actual public components/providers with host-owned state, including interaction, reset, live theme/window/fixture changes and async replacement as applicable. Mock only native/external seams; no snapshot or implementation-mirroring test substitutes for public behavior. Resolve attributed regressions.

**Closing evidence:** Exact named test cases/commands and source revision; passing focused suites, owning format/lint/type gates, app composition checks and meaningful negative cases. Unrelated broad suites are not rerun without invalidation.

<a id="immutable-consumer-and-build"></a>

#### 3.1.6. Immutable Consumer And Build

Evidence key: `G06`.

**Pass condition:** Build/pack the changed native package; freeze a fresh outside-workspace consumer using only its archive and declared host peers. Frozen reinstall and strict consumer types pass. Both Metro platform bundles/maps resolve to the recorded archive/fixture with no workspace/sibling source links, duplicate React or unintended DOM/web/runtime dependencies.

**Closing evidence:** Source/file hashes, archive SHA, package/lock/peer identities, consumer location and commands, iOS/Android bundle/map module assertions and exact compiled public exports. Older proof is reused only after the relevant byte/scope identity is verified.

<a id="installed-android"></a>

#### 3.1.7. Installed Android

Evidence key: `G07`.

**Pass condition:** Every applicable scenario in 3.2.1–3.2.12 passes on actual installed Release output across profiles 3.3.1–3.3.8. Record native callback/owner agreement, first activation and measured geometry/keyboard/window postconditions; preserve failed first attempts and attributable fixes.

**Closing evidence:** Android cell records source/archive/bundle/map/built/installed hashes, app/build ID, emulator/runtime/window/fontScale/theme, exact fixture/inspector, expected versus observed callbacks/frames and capture/log locations. A successful build or mock does not pass this gate.

<a id="installed-ios"></a>

#### 3.1.8. Installed iOS

Evidence key: `G08`.

**Pass condition:** The same applicable scenario 3.2.1–3.2.12/profile 3.3.1–3.3.8 matrix passes on the approved x86_64 simulator Release lane. Real observer callbacks are required before interpreting an editor/interaction verdict; healthy process/control handshakes are insufficient. Preserve exact first/warm conditions and immutable failure cohorts.

**Closing evidence:** iOS identities and evidence fields matching section 3.1.7, with observer/method/callback proof. Actual failed/blocked cells remain open even when unrelated cells or a repeated query pass. iPhone signing/ARM/physical proof is excluded only under the existing owner deferral.

<a id="review-and-docs-and-closure"></a>

#### 3.1.9. Review, Documentation And Closure

Evidence key: `G09`.

**Pass condition:** Resolve technical findings for the reviewed revision, document public use and explicit native equivalents/limits, maintain the scene and catalogue/matrix, and commit coherent changes through normal hooks. No unresolved mandatory capability or installed cell is promoted as accepted.

**Closing evidence:** Recorded technical review scope/revision/findings/resolution, current curated reference/scene, passing affected docs build and normal commit checks, clean/understood Git state, and a cell-by-cell completion audit. Physical/owner-visual deferrals stay linked to their authority.

<a id="native-scenario-matrix"></a>

### 3.2. Native Scenario Matrix

Scenario evidence keys `S01`–`S12` map in order to the numbered rows 3.2.1–3.2.12 below. Each applicable scenario is instantiated as `CAT.<manifest-id>.<platform>.<profile>.S<n>.<case>` with its setup, one action, exact expected native/owner/callback/geometry outcome and evidence. Enumerate supported variants instead of testing one representative while claiming the whole set. A scenario that does not apply to the canonical/accepted native contract records `N/A` with the reason; unavailable tools record `BLOCKED`. Native first-activation and focused editor outcomes cannot be replaced by paced typing, blur-only observations or source mocks.

| Scenario                                 | Mandatory cases / exact acceptance outcome                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 3.2.1. Initial/default/controlled values | Every accepted empty/false/zero/null value, initial defaults and programmatic owner changes render correctly. For each controlled axis, accept then repeatedly decline a user request; authoritative value remains unchanged on declines, with exact requested callback arguments/counts.                                                                               |
| 3.2.2. Activation/guards/cancellation    | First visible action succeeds exactly once; same-value suppression, disabled/read-only/pending/member guards and cancelled/interrupted press behavior follow the component contract. Test each actual action target and the accepted minimum-target/explicit-constraint rules.                                                                                          |
| 3.2.3. Native editing/format/commit      | For editable controls, freshly focused unpaced input agrees across native field, native change, callback and committed owner while focused; final submit/edit events preserve the final text. Include empty/correction/selection/multiline/numeric locale/draft/commit/step cases from the contract. An earlier failure stays failed; a warm repeat is a separate case. |
| 3.2.4. Collections and async replacement | Empty and populated collections, typed keys, relabel/reorder/removal/reinsertion, disabled keys and caller-filtered/async replacement preserve identity/value and valid callback counts. Virtualized families open near the tail of 200 variable-height items with the selected row fully viewable and bounded measurement-progress retries.                            |
| 3.2.5. Presentation/keyboard/context     | Open/close/reopen, programmatic transitions, repeated owner veto, Back/Close/escape and nested supported window chains preserve one active owner, live caller/theme context and local draft/count. A visible result/confirm/Close action works on its first activation while the keyboard is active; an explicit finish-editing comparison cannot substitute.           |
| 3.2.6. Teardown/focus restoration        | Close, ancestor close, replacement, owner/trigger/ref removal and unmount cancel queued work; surviving refs receive only valid restoration requests. No stale callback, implicit business action, phantom value mutation or reopened keyboard. Preserve state for covered mounted content and documented unmount semantics.                                            |
| 3.2.7. Theme/color/state                 | Both light/dark, every supported order/mode/validation state and priority, all default/custom icon/content/empty-copy cases and mounted theme updates preserve values and expected owned colors. Record native target/semantic state separately from pixel review.                                                                                                      |
| 3.2.8. Geometry/dimensions/slots         | All supported geometries/sizes/orientations/directions, authored numeric/percentage/zero/auto dimensions where accepted, each style slot and final override precedence produce the expected actual frames/owned presentation. Explicit constraints remain real and documented.                                                                                          |
| 3.2.9. Adaptive window/large text        | At profiles 3.3.1–3.3.6, long English/French labels/content and actions remain reachable inside safe-area/system-bar/keyboard bounds. Scroll to the actual end and measure the bottom action/content; an unscrolled screenshot is insufficient. Portrait → landscape → portrait retains running-scene state; font-change app recreation is recorded separately.         |
| 3.2.10. Native accessibility bindings    | Inspect owned labels/roles/value/checked/selected/disabled/busy traits, accessible target count and supported focus-request targets. Decorative content does not become duplicate controls. Physical spoken output, gestures and hardware keyboard/IME acceptance retain their explicit device-lane deferral.                                                           |
| 3.2.11. Motion/gestures                  | For every supported motion/gesture: verify completion/cancellation, direction, current authoritative state, system Reduce Motion changes, teardown and no post-unmount work. Unsupported gestures are documented contract dispositions, not silently inferred from a web implementation.                                                                                |
| 3.2.12. Maintained host lifecycle        | Reset explicitly restores defaults; theme/window/fixture changes preserve active-scene state. Use actual host dimensions, normal/large text and synthetic blocked-region fixtures. Do not treat synthetic gaps as hardware fold/posture or Activity-recreation persistence proof.                                                                                       |

<a id="required-native-profiles"></a>

### 3.3. Required Native Profiles

Profile evidence keys `P01`–`P08` map in order to the numbered rows 3.3.1–3.3.8 below. Apply these on both approved installed Release platforms to each affected scenario, recording actual dimensions/scales rather than guessing equivalent iOS categories. Profiles 3.3.1–3.3.6 form the layout acceptance grid; repeat the relevant state/customization variants from scenarios 3.2.7–3.2.8. Profiles 3.3.7–3.3.8 add host constraint compositions. Do not multiply non-layout cases arbitrarily, but record why a profile does not affect a particular scenario; reductions in coverage need a contract-specific reason.

| Profile | Orientation/text/window                           | Required observation                                                                                                    |
| ------- | ------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 3.3.1.  | Normal portrait, normal system text               | Baseline values/actions/roles and actual safe/window/keyboard frames.                                                   |
| 3.3.2.  | Normal landscape, normal system text              | Reachable content/actions, correct direction/layout and state retention through rotation.                               |
| 3.3.3.  | Portrait, approximately 1.5× system text          | Actual measured fontScale/category; long content and end-of-scroll action inside visible safe bounds.                   |
| 3.3.4.  | Landscape, approximately 1.5× system text         | Same reachability and geometry with the shorter keyboard/window viewport.                                               |
| 3.3.5.  | Portrait, approximately 2.0× system text          | Record the platform's actual available category/scale; inspect the full enlarged content and last action.               |
| 3.3.6.  | Landscape, approximately 2.0× system text         | No lost actions, clipped mandatory content or state loss from running-window changes.                                   |
| 3.3.7.  | Compact and expanded host constraints             | Use actual bounded host widths; compact/expanded fixtures cannot fabricate a larger display or a safe touch region.     |
| 3.3.8.  | Vertical and horizontal synthetic blocked regions | Values/context survive layout changes and actions remain in their live panes; this does not certify real fold hardware. |

<a id="execution-queue-and-approval-boundary"></a>

### 3.4. Execution Queue And Approval Boundary

Dependency-ready construction proceeds across visual/content/media primitives, selection/tags/toggles, progress/loaders/counter/form composition, range controls, links/navigation/feedback, overlay/menu/panel, tables, searchable multi-selection and date/time families. Audit each family rather than preselect a headless dependency. Existing source components also receive missing capability/assertion mappings and affected acceptance work. Novel navigation/overlay/collection/date semantics or dependency/theme/tooling changes require a concrete decision, while unrelated rows continue. Component contracts and results live here and in experiments.md; do not create another proposal/receipt document for each family.

<a id="standing-construction-policy-and-next-contracts"></a>

### 3.5. Standing Construction Policy And Next Contracts

**Authorized by Gabriel on 2026-10-17 for the full goal:** proceed through the entire 53-component construction queue in Agentic Execution Mode with logical commits, using canonical web contracts and established native equivalents. Ordinary ports retain supported prop names/defaults, authoritative values/callback ordering, full customization and explicit types. DOM dimensions/colors/classes/events/refs map to native logical dimensions/ColorValue/style slots/native event payloads/native refs. This authorization covers those conventional public native counterparts without another permission stop for each component. It does not authorize silently dropping inherited capabilities, broad refactors, new dependencies/tooling/theme contracts, general navigation/overlay/collection/date architecture, registry/CLI/distribution or publication. Concrete new decisions remain owner-reviewed while other rows continue.

Indicator/LineSegment and CircleLoader/LaggingLinesLoader now have audited canonical mappings and committed native source/maintained scenes. Avatar now has the mapped contract, source/scene and immutable consumer proof in 3.5.5. Card is audited in 3.5.6 and retains a shared native-motion architecture decision; the independent standalone Tag contract follows in 3.5.7. These completed display/loading ports add no dependency, text editor or overlay.

| Component                       | Approved public contract and exact canonical/native mapping                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Indicator                       | Native View ref; optional `isActive` defaults true and remains visual caller-owned state with no change/default state API. `size` defaults 10 native logical units; `borderWidth` defaults 0 and is finite/nonnegative. `shape` circle/rounded/square retains constants/as-const array/type. `color` defaults to the owned native content foreground or theme foreground; `inactiveColor` defaults transparent. Inactive fill changes independently from border, whose default remains the active color. Optional explicit `borderColor` wins. Native dimensions/ColorValue replace CSS unit/currentColor strings.                                                                                                                     |
| Indicator customization         | Keep `customStyles` and final `style` precedence, plus native style arrays/registered styles and theme updates. A native style callback receives authoritative `isActive` and post-custom `defaultStyle`. Circle/rounded/square map to existing round/rounded/zero radius. Decorative by default; explicit native accessibility/name/layout/identity props remain available. Own props never leak to View.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| LineSegment                     | Native View ref; defaults direction horizontal, height/width 50 and size 2.5; color uses existing controlBorder. Preserve canonical tested geometry exactly: the historical horizontal token means height × size, while vertical means size × width. Do not silently swap these despite the visual naming surprise. Native dimensions replace CSS lengths; zero and percentage dimensions remain meaningful. Constants, as-const array and derived direction type retain canonical names.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| LineSegment customization       | Keep root computed → customStyles → final style precedence, native ColorValue/style validation and native ID/name/layout props; own wrapper props are consumed. A native style callback receives direction and post-custom defaultStyle. Decorative by default with explicit native accessibility opt-in; no children or uncontrolled/value API. DOM classes/refs/CSS units are executable native type exclusions under the conventional mapping, not ignored props.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Exact initial CAP/STYLE mapping | Indicator's five root cases cover default active/circle/10/0/currentColor; inactive fill plus unchanged active border; explicit color/border/size/rounded plus custom/final margin/background precedence; square/string-size native equivalent; and wrapper consumption. LineSegment's eleven root cases cover render; default height50/size2.5; explicit horizontal height80/size4/red; vertical width50/size2.5 and width100/size5/blue; class-to-native-style equivalence; computed/custom/final precedence; custom opacity/radius; custom color override; native size equivalent; and wrapper consumption. Each becomes a stable `CAP`/`STYLE` row and public assertion, with exact native setup/outcome and canonical test names. |

Under the standing policy, a completed display pair is only an intermediate source/consumer checkpoint. Its acceptance gates 3.1.6–3.1.9 and applicable installed cells remain required; the agent immediately chooses the next dependency-ready incomplete family rather than completing the overall goal or waiting for a routine next-work prompt.

Exact source outcomes close only their own gates; 3.1.4 native customization, 3.1.7/3.1.8 installed profiles and 3.1.9 closure remain open until real evidence exists.

| ------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| CAP.avatar.001–.005 | Render, size, three geometries, raised state and explicit colors | Default size 30, round geometry and inverse theme foreground/background; numeric and numeric-string logical sizes, percentages/auto and explicit zero. Existing theme radii/shadow recipe, foreground before legacy color, live provider updates. CSS units/variables use native dimensions/styles instead.                                                                                             |
| CAP.avatar.006      | Image URL and fallback                                           | A real core Image mounts to receive load/error events; only a successfully loaded image becomes visible. Missing/empty URL and errors show the eligible fallback. URI replacement starts a fresh image lifecycle; stale callbacks from a replaced/unmounted image cannot change the current content. Default resizeMode is contain.                                                                     |
| CAP.avatar.007–.008 | Name initials and localized anonymous labels                     | Preserve canonical first-code-unit uppercase behavior and name-presence rules for both names, one name, no names and empty names; defaults Name/Unknown. Empty localized strings remain empty. Native root name precedence is accessibilityLabel → aria-label → resolved full name.                                                                                                                     |
| CAP.avatar.009–.011 | Four style owners, final root precedence and custom text props   | Root computed → customStyles → final style/callback with post-custom defaultStyle; root/fallback color reaches owned Text. Image and fallback styles are independent; text customTextProps.customStyles → customTextStyles → customTextProps.style. Native registered/array styles and dynamic colors remain supported. Image visibility and decorative content semantics stay owned.                   |
| CAP.avatar.012      | Wrapper consumption and native forwarding                        | Consume every calibration/content alias; actual View ref, nativeID/testID/name precedence and original native layout/press payloads remain available. DOM class/asChild/events are rejected by public types and use existing native boundary dispositions. Owned graphic children cannot be replaced.                                                                                                   |
| NATIVE.avatar.001   | Radix fallback delay and image lifetime                          | Undefined delay is immediately eligible; defined delay (including zero) schedules eligibility once. Changing URI/name/theme never restarts that lifetime; changing/removing a pending delay cancels its old timer, and an already eligible fallback stays eligible. Unmount cancels queued work. Finite timer values follow native timer clamping.                                                      |
| NATIVE.avatar.002   | Guarded activation and coherent semantics                        | Only clickable plus onPress enables activation; native disabled also guards/cancels it through the existing interaction helper. One named root is an image or button; image/fallback/text are decorative. Accessible=false supports decorative composition. Actual size stays caller-owned; measured automatic hitSlop follows the existing 44pt/48dp target and explicit-hitSlop/clipped-parent rules. |

Fallback text keeps native font scaling enabled by default; customTextProps can set supported native text typography/scaling and its own final style without replacing the initials or introducing another action target. Inherited browser asChild/DOM span behavior uses the existing native host boundary rather than a new arbitrary-host API. Instantiate applicable 3.2.1/3.2.2/3.2.6–3.2.10/3.2.12 cases on both platforms and affected 3.3 profiles, including first load, error, replacement/removal, delayed fallback, theme updates, explicit zero, all four style owners and minimum-target constraints. Editing, collection and overlay scenarios are N/A because Avatar owns none of those behaviors; required installed and physical evidence remains separate from source mocks.

#### 3.5.6. Card Motion Contract Decision

Card's complete source/helpers/CSS/types/root suite and pinned Motion/Framer Motion 12.40.0 feature/type definitions are audited. Its three root tests cover root aliases/identity, dimensions/radius and wrapper consumption. The owned contract also has column layout, spacing-unit ×3 gap/padding, default radius10, default raised=true and layoutMode=size/position. A caller-supplied inherited layout prop is overwritten by layoutMode in the actual wrapper; do not advertise that ignored upstream prop as a working capability. All other inherited Motion groups below reach motion.div and remain required.

| Capability key     | Inherited contract and native decision needed                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| CAP.card.animation | initial/animate/exit/variants/transition, legacy controls, custom variant data/inheritance and live values need one explicit native animation/control contract, with current-value interruption and complete lifecycle callbacks. Existing component-local Animated loops do not provide that public contract.    |
| CAP.card.gestures  | Tap/start/cancel, hover/focus variants, pan sessions and drag/start/end/lock/transition callbacks require coherent native event payloads and responder cancellation. Native drag constraints, elasticity, momentum, controls, listener/propagation and snap-to-origin need a reviewed gesture ownership model.    |
| CAP.card.viewport  | whileInView, enter/leave and viewport options depend on actual visibility within scroll/root/margin/amount/once conditions, not merely onLayout. Native ancestor scroll/viewport observation needs a shared contract.                                                                                             |
| CAP.card.layout    | layoutMode, layoutId, layoutDependency, layoutScroll/root/anchor/crossfade and measure/start/complete events depend on projection/presence/shared-layout coordination. A local x/y transform cannot establish all these capabilities.                                                                             |
| CAP.card.advanced  | transformTemplate, live values and inherited custom/context controls need native equivalents; DOM/Framer marker attributes and browser nodes remain the existing host-boundary disposition. The CSS-only style/ReactNode children overrides in Card do not independently expose MotionStyle/MotionValue children. |

This is a consolidated architecture decision, not permission to drop capabilities or certify a static Card. Keep CAT.card and its required motion cells OPEN and source construction pending while independent rows proceed. The next reviewable proposal must specify the native animation/gesture/presence/viewport/layout API, implementation ownership and exact mappings for every inherited group, then identify whether core RN suffices or a separately approved dependency is necessary. No generic motion runtime, dependency or truncated Card is introduced by this audit.

#### 3.5.8. Link Capability And Scenario Matrix

Source checkpoint: native `9168796`, maintained scene/reference `f36c9df`, immutable consumer `CP-27`. The complete canonical Link component/helpers/CSS/index, eleven root tests, anchor types, accessibility aliases and receipt verifier are audited. Link is a portable anchor without router/Next coupling. The native counterpart uses core Pressable/Linking, existing Text/content/interaction/target/theme/style patterns and component-local core Animated; it adds no router, dependency, theme role or global history store. Required href is preserved verbatim, including empty strings, so an owner can intercept relative/fragment/empty targets through onPress.preventDefault(). Otherwise one enabled activation calls Linking.openURL once after the original native onPress callback; synchronous throws and asynchronous rejections reach onNavigationError(error, href), or a diagnostic warning when no handler exists. Disabled/cancelled gestures never call the owner or navigation. Async rejection reporting belongs to the originating activation and is suppressed after unmount; changing href does not relabel an earlier failure.

This independent leaf precedes ThumbnailShowcase and introduces no media adapter, dependency, theme role or shared image/cache architecture. The native public surface retains src/srcCandidates/alt/fallback/onError/renderImage and ordinary native Image props/styles/events. Source candidates are caller-owned strings, not native source objects; source is consumed/typed out so it cannot silently replace the candidate model. Default rendering uses core Image; injected renderImage receives alt/src/imageProps/onError with native Image props and the same error handler identity. Neither canonical nor native wrapper exposes a universal root ref because an injected renderer/fallback can return arbitrary content; an injected renderer may own its actual ref.

#### 3.5.15. Native Drawing Dependency Decision

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-17 through the explicit SVG dependency decision.** Metadata key: `DEC.native-svg.01`. The complete CircularProgress/Path/Counter source, helpers, CSS/compatibility, indexes and root suites are audited; the focused baseline passes all14 CircularProgress and10 Counter cases. The owned arc, dash, line-cap, SVG text and six independent style surfaces need an actual native vector renderer. Core View border/clipping approximations would narrow that contract. The installed Expo57.0.26 bundledNativeModules.json explicitly selects react-native-svg15.15.4; that exact upstream tag exists and declares React/React Native peers. Its [upstream package manifest](https://github.com/software-mansion/react-native-svg/blob/v15.15.4/package.json) and [Fabric/install documentation](https://github.com/software-mansion/react-native-svg/blob/main/README.md) are reference evidence, not proof that the local native lane passes.

| ---------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 3.5.15.1. Approved native peer     | Add exact react-native-svg15.15.4 as a required peer/dev dependency of packages/react-native and a direct dependency of apps/native-showcase. Hosts own the linked native module, as with existing native peers. Keep every existing pin unchanged. Record only the new peer/importer/transitive dependency additions in pnpm-lock.yaml; do not update unrelated resolved versions. No Expo dependency enters the reusable package.                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 3.5.15.2. Renderer boundary        | Use native Svg/Path/Circle/Text primitives for the existing100×100 viewBox, two-arc path, signed dash offset, butt/round/square caps, background and centered text. Root remains a native View ref with implemented ProgressBar semantics. Retain root, SVG, track, path, text and background customization with explicit native style/presentation types and final precedence. Preserve caller values and existing palette; this approves no new theme role or generic motion/navigation layer.                                                                                                                                                          |
| 3.5.15.3. Canonical behavior       | Preserve clamped ratio/invalid-range-zero behavior, conditional background padding, actual stroke default6 versus helper-radius default8, circleRatio multiplication, current counter-clockwise path/dash behavior, owned children and truthy text/background gates. Counter keeps raw string/number values, truthy numeric0 versus string0 max gates, rounded percentage thresholds with danger precedence and independent root/text/indicator styles. Audit browser SVG-style/fill quirks and nonfinite derived geometry explicitly before renderer implementation; do not fabricate a normalized business value or silently remove valid counter text. |
| 3.5.15.4. Dependency-free work now | Conventional private circular geometry and Counter presentation models may be implemented/tested without SVG imports, package edits or public component exports. They are implementation preparation, count as no new counterpart, close no native cell and do not approve the dependency. Exact source assertion mappings remain in the evidence companion.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| 3.5.15.5. After approval           | Implement CircularProgress then Counter under the existing full goal, with all applicable rubric/matrix cells retained. Run native component/provider/slot/lifecycle and public-type checks, package build, maintained scenes/reference, fresh immutable consumer with the new exact host peer and both native graphs. Allow the approved SVG native runtime while still rejecting workspace/sibling/DOM/web/test or duplicate React paths. Native module changes require fresh local Release products before installed acceptance; they do not authorize another startup investigation or iPhone signing.                                                |

The owner explicitly approves exact react-native-svg15.15.4 as the reusable native peer/dev dependency and showcase dependency, enabling the pair under the existing full goal. This satisfies AGENTS.md's dependency/public-API boundary and the standing construction policy; all existing pins remain unchanged. It does not reopen ordinary per-family approval. Panel backdrop blur, TagGroup's distinct collection-member composition and the already recorded Card motion architecture remain separate concrete decisions; no truncated counterpart is certified while they are unresolved. Installed Android/iOS, native rendering, inherited capabilities, closer owner/device authority and all existing failures retain their current rubric disposition. Preserve the browser quirk: named pathColor/trackColor set color, backgroundColor enables its circle and sets backgroundColor; explicit stroke/fill defaults still own paint. #### 3.5.17. Shared Native Floating Layer Decision

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-18 after the requested summary.** The approval covers exact teleport1.2.2, the shared per-window host/trigger/lifetime architecture and conventional dependent-family construction. Native focus/key feature-flag changes remain a separate decision. Metadata key: `DEC.native-floating.01`. This is one shared construction decision for ClickPopover, Tooltip, HoverPopover and Menu, then their owned uses in Breadcrumbs, Pagination, CompactTypeaheadSearch, TagComboBox and date/filtering families after each complete audit. It approves no capability removal and does not replace required installed acceptance with a source pass. Source counterparts stay35/53 while the foundation is prepared.

The complete ClickPopover component/helpers/CSS/index/root suite, pinned Popover/position/dismissal/DialogTrigger declarations and native overlay provider/registry are inspected. The unmodified focused baseline passes72 cases: ClickPopover13, Tooltip10, HoverPopover14 and Menu35. Tooltip/HoverPopover owned and inherited audits are completed below; Menu remains baseline evidence rather than a complete family audit. ClickPopover owns dimensions, rounded/orthogonal geometry, raised defaulttrue, color/order, optional10-unit arrow, optional Dialog child, three style owners and entry/exit recipes; inherited contracts include controlled/default open,22 logical/physical placement forms, offset/crossOffset/containerPadding/flip, anchor/boundary/scroll refs, maxHeight, live positioning, outside veto, keyboard dismissal, nonmodal behavior, focus and lifecycle. Root custom/final styles and arrow/dialog owners retain their original precedence and color/paint quirks. None is silently removed to obtain a native counterpart.

| Decision part                                | Concrete proposed contract and verification                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| -------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| 3.5.17.1. Native portal peer                 | Add exact react-native-teleport1.2.2 as required peer/dev dependency of packages/react-native and direct showcase dependency. Registry publication is2026-09-10T12:03:32.242Z, older than the unchanged seven-day buffer. Audit archive matches registry SHA-512 and upstream gitHead88eabb867bb929cdbaca4a0c210499c137246019. There are no runtime dependencies; Fabric codegen/native Android/iOS sources require a fresh linked binary. Existing React/RN/SVG/safe-area/Intl and all other pins remain unchanged. No package scripts run during the audit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 3.5.17.2. React DOM peer cost                | Upstream declares react, react-native and react-dom as nonoptional wildcard peers. The native entry resolves .native branches; .web/default browser branches import React DOM. This proposal explicitly accepts the transitive peer metadata without adding React DOM to Codon's own native peer/runtime contract. Fresh consumer proofs must show that platform-native graphs contain no React DOM/RN Web/browser entry. Existing standalone consumer's exact react-dom19.2.3 development fixture can satisfy upstream for tests; workspace's existing19.2.7 web resolution is not upgraded or repinned. Do not hide a new peer mismatch or silently patch upstream metadata; resolve/report it before accepting the lock.                                                                                                   |
| 3.5.17.3. Host API                           | Add NativeFloatingLayerHost with native View ref, children, native style and actual onLayout. It owns a full-size native container and a per-instance unique portal host; callers mount it at a native window root. Its layout is explicit, avoiding an implicit wrapper change to NativeOverlayProvider. Portal content stays in its original React tree; arbitrary caller contexts/state/callbacks must survive native reparenting. Root host must not intercept unrelated touches. Existing Modal/AlertDialog/Select native windows gain a private local host inside their existing window, so a nested layer cannot escape into a different native window. Portal implementation stays private behind Codon ownership.                                                                                                    |
| 3.5.17.4. Window and layer lifetimes         | Preserve existing single-branch native-window registry and native onShow/onDismiss barriers. A separate private host-local floating stack tracks logical parentage, modal/nonmodal semantics, active pointer/focus ownership and entry/exit generations. Native Back/Escape/accessibility escape first requests dismissal of the active eligible layer, then the existing window; closed/covered/retired callbacks cannot act. Removing a parent closes its descendants, retires measurements/timers, restores only a live eligible trigger and does not fabricate controlled callbacks. No competing native-window behavior is relaxed incidentally.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| 3.5.17.5. Placement model                    | Private dependency-free rectangle calculations cover22 placement strings, live LTR/RTL resolution, offsets, flip/shift, arrow edge clamping, host/boundary/keyboard intersections and constrained maxHeight. Anchor/native window coordinates are measured into the host's coordinate system; no guessed screen offset. Measurements carry host/anchor/content/open generations and ignore stale results after replacement, scroll, resize, keyboard/profile change, close/reopen or unmount. shouldUpdatePosition=false freezes only the established position, not controlled state. Do not normalize caller values or overwrite authored zero/false. Initial private geometry preparation can proceed under standing construction authority; it exports no public component and certifies no portal/native behavior.        |
| 3.5.17.6. Trigger and dismissal boundary     | Native triggerRef/boundaryRef/scrollRef replace DOM Element refs. Expose a conventional NativeDialogTrigger native equivalent with controlled isOpen/onOpenChange or one-time defaultOpen and explicit render-state trigger props/ref, avoiding implicit mutation of arbitrary children. Render props provide the original native event and authoritative open state. Outside-interaction veto receives a typed native target/event record; no fabricated DOM Element. Modal layers isolate accessibility and focus; nonmodal layers retain background interaction and explicit description/trigger semantics. Hover/tooltip timing and menu collection behavior are audited per family; touch-only behavior is not misrepresented as hover.                                                                                  |
| 3.5.17.7. First construction and propagation | Implement the host plus complete ClickPopover contract first, then Tooltip/HoverPopover/Menu and dependency-ready Breadcrumbs/Pagination under the standing full goal. Retain all inherited capability cells and exact native equivalents. Three ClickPopover style owners, both geometries, five existing orders, optional arrow/Dialog, native refs/events, entry/exit/Reduce Motion and caller-owned values need direct source/type/scene proof. Keep theme roles/palette, dependency ownership, publication/CI and iPhone-signing policy unchanged.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 3.5.17.8. Required acceptance                | Prove isolated host contexts, mount order, clipping escape, host replacement, parent/child/modal/nonmodal pointer and accessibility behavior, native focus restoration, fresh press delivery, anchor movement/keyboard/window/RTL/font profiles, stale callbacks and teardown. Compile the exact native module with pinned RN0.86.3 for both local Release platforms; upstream compatibility claims or source API matches do not pass that gate. Fresh packed consumer/compiled types and both native graphs must prove one React/RN graph, exact portal/SVG peers and no browser/test/workspace/sibling paths. Installed matrix evidence must match source/build/inspector identities. A module compile failure is investigated within this dependency integration, not by restarting old XCTest/LLDB transport experiments. |

#### 3.5.21. Native Interaction And Viewport Backend Decision

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-18: “Approve the source-owned backend plan.”** Metadata key: `DEC.native-input.01`. The approval covers the source-owned native adapter, codegen/autolinking and explicit focus/key contracts below, without new dependencies or SDK flags. The approved floating architecture now has private ClickPopover/Tooltip/HoverPopover source, but generic native keyboard focus, complete key transport, tooltip description linkage, ancestor-scroll closure and visible keyboard geometry remain mandatory gaps. This source-owned native adapter belongs inside packages/react-native. All53 catalogue rows and every acceptance cell remain in scope; this is a foundation decision under the full goal, not a smaller replacement goal.

The read-only audit finds no iOS key producer in the inspected published RN0.86.3 UIKit/Fabric/ReactApple view sources. Default-false focus/key/Pressability-hover flags and private provider initialization cannot by themselves establish a complete two-platform backend. The [published react-native-external-keyboard candidate](https://github.com/ArturKalach/react-native-external-keyboard) has a pin-compatible1.2.1 line; latest2.0.1 requires RN>=0.87 and is excluded. Published1.2.1 is not a drop-in solution: native key records lose Command/Meta information, the default ref is a Proxy whose focus invokes both keyboard and screen-reader focus, and two actual published-hook reproductions show a queued40ms press calling an unmounted or replaced owner. These findings are recorded in [evidence3.31.4](experiments.md#3314-native-input-source-audit-and-backend-proposal). No package is installed, no package script runs, and no native flag or product source changes during the audit.

| Decision part                                       | Approved contract and limit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 3.5.21.1. Ownership and dependency policy           | Add the minimal Objective-C++/UIKit and Java/Android native implementation to the existing native package, with private RN codegen facades. Keep all current peers, dependency versions, palette, Node/pnpm/TypeScript presets, CI/publication/CLI behavior and sibling repos unchanged. Do not add external-keyboard as a dependency or ship its high-level HOCs, debounce driver, Proxy ref or singleton trap provider. Audited MIT native sources may inform/adapt low-level implementation with required license notices. No RN source patch, feature-flag override, release-channel change or force-reset API is authorized by this approved route.                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 3.5.21.2. Native package/artifact boundary          | Add native source directories, one uniquely named codegen specification, a podspec and Android/autolinking metadata; include exactly their source/config inputs in the native package archive while excluding build outputs. Use the existing pinned RN/Expo codegen/autolinking and local Release build pipeline. This makes @codon-ui/react-native a hybrid native package and requires linked consumer rebuilds. A fresh outside-workspace archive/install/type/graph/native-build proof is mandatory before accepting that boundary. This is source/package construction only; publication and CI changes remain separate.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| 3.5.21.3. Actual refs and programmatic focus        | Provide NativeFocusView as an actual native View host with normal View props/ref/measurement semantics and no extra layout wrapper; provide focusNativeTarget(target) and blurNativeTarget(target) requests for registered native targets and unchanged native TextInputs. Existing root/editor refs remain their actual native instances, never Proxy handles. Generic upstream View.focus() retains upstream behavior; the new helper is the explicit portable keyboard-focus equivalent and is used by owned labels, overlay entry/restoration and roving controls. Unregistered plain core Views are not silently treated as successful targets: custom focus targets use NativeFocusView. Requests and actual focus acknowledgments are distinct; no accessibility-focus notification substitutes for keyboard focus.                                   |
| 3.5.21.4. Original key data and public press types  | Define TNativeKeyboardEvent with actual native target, logical key plus raw platform code/characters, phase/repeat, separate Shift/Alt/Control/Meta/Caps fields, original modifier data, timestamp and native consumption result. Extend activation callbacks to TNativePressEvent = existing GestureResponderEvent or the actual native keyboard event; never cast keyboard input into a touch event or manufacture coordinates. Existing pointer event objects/callbacks remain unchanged. Callers that read touch-only fields narrow the union. Focus/pointer origin comes from actual native input; a generic focus callback is not automatically keyboard-visible. Native pointer events can retain the current mouse callback contract because the pinned NativePointerEvent extends NativeMouseEvent.                                                 |
| 3.5.21.5. Native key/default policy                 | Add a private per-window key scope with declarative capture policy for the audited family keys, modifier guards and editable-target exclusions. Native consumption happens before asynchronous JS delivery; a later JS preventDefault cannot retroactively cancel UIKit/Android default handling. Expose that actual result and make custom cancellation use the declared native key policy rather than pretending browser-synchronous cancellation exists. Preserve canonical callback/owner-request ordering in the JS policy, native press start/end/cancellation and exact one-action delivery; retire key repeats/held presses and pending requests on focus loss, guard takeover, scope replacement and unmount. No40ms debounce or delayed old-owner action.                                                                                          |
| 3.5.21.6. Window/layer integration                  | Reuse NativeFloatingLayerHost and the existing native-window/floating registries for logical ownership, active leaf, onShow/onDismiss barriers and generation admission. The private native scope occupies the host's existing root boundary; it must not add an implicit consumer layout wrapper. Modal focus remains in the active eligible logical branch, nonmodal descriptions retain parent/background actions, and covered/closed/retired nodes cannot act. Bind owned controls through the pinned Pressability behavior and the actual native focus host while preserving every existing min/explicit-zero geometry, style slot, root ref and caller value. Core TextInput controlled values/edit events remain untouched; do not replace the text pipeline or claim RN-TEXT-01/ComboBox repaired.                                                   |
| 3.5.21.7. Native description binding                | Link the actual trigger/description native targets privately while the measured description is active. Preserve caller label, value and hint. Use AndroidX native tooltip-text metadata and iOS AXCustomContent/AXCustomContentProvider supplementary content, with explicit lifecycle identity and availability handling; do not overwrite hints, synthesize DOM nodes or move screen-reader focus. Native description updates follow the rendered native label/content and retire on replacement/close; native binding inspection remains required, with physical spoken output under its existing owner deferral. These are approved native equivalents, not an existing pass or a promise that browser ARIA is identical.                                                                                                                                |
| 3.5.21.8. Measured viewport and scroll transport    | Observe only live registered native anchors, scroll owners and windows. iOS window/keyboard notifications and real ancestor scroll offsets, plus Android actual window/inset/scroll observations, produce host-relative visible bounds with generation/coordinate-space identity. Intersect with the actually measured host to avoid guessed screen origins or double keyboard subtraction. Actual ancestor scrolling requests tooltip closure; internal content scrolling remains separate. For ClickPopover scrollRef, capture the real focused-descendant/viewport/offset anchor before repositioning and restore against the new native layout only if the scroll/focus/host generation is still current and the user has not scrolled meanwhile. No source-injected dimensions, inner-content-as-outer-box substitution or controlled text/value write. |
| 3.5.21.9. Incremental implementation and acceptance | First commit the raw protocol/native hosts and exact source/public compile assertions, then focus/key/description/scroll binding, then migrate owned controls and floating families at coherent boundaries. Prove actual View refs, full modifier information, current callbacks, zero dimensions, original pointer events, balanced press state, native window barriers and teardown before broad propagation. Build one matching local Release product per platform for each coherent integration revision, with unsigned iOS Simulator and frozen inputs. Run actual installed input/geometry/description forms only through a working authorized transport; compilation/source passes never close them. Preserve all7306 supplemental cells and the full53-component rubric/matrices, adding exact new backend forms rather than deleting gaps.          |
| 3.5.21.10. Existing blockers and authority          | This approval grants no XCTest/LLDB/SystemUI repair, guest reset, repeated startup replay, iPhone signing, physical-lane un-deferral, dependency upgrade, push, merge or publication. Keep old failing cells and sealed cohorts immutable. If installed observation remains blocked, keep required cells OPEN/BLOCKED with the actual reason and continue independent construction. The full goal ends only after every mandatory cell is closed under its real evidence tier.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Approved public boundary:

```ts
export type TNativePressEvent = GestureResponderEvent | TNativeKeyboardEvent
export type TNativeKeyboardData = Readonly<{
  kind: "keyboard"
  key: string
  nativeCode: number
  characters: string
  phase: "down" | "up"
  repeat: boolean
  shiftKey: boolean
  altKey: boolean
  ctrlKey: boolean
  metaKey: boolean
  capsLock: boolean
  nativeModifierData: string
  nativeDefaultHandled: boolean
}>
export type TNativeKeyboardEvent = NativeSyntheticEvent<TNativeKeyboardData>
export const NativeFocusView: NativeViewComponentWithActualViewRef
export const focusNativeTarget: (target: View | TextInput | null) => boolean
export const blurNativeTarget: (target: View | TextInput | null) => boolean
```

The boolean helper result means request admission, not completed focus; completion is proven by the native event/target. The sketch's NativeViewComponentWithActualViewRef denotes the ordinary typed View-props/ref boundary, not a new opaque imperative handle. Native key scope/description/viewport/scroll facades remain private. This is a new native-equivalent contract: existing measurement/editor refs and caller state remain stable, while programmatic non-text focus and keyboard press events gain explicit APIs. The main cost is owning and validating a small two-platform native transport layer and shipping its native source inputs.

Alternative for owner consideration: use exact1.2.1 only as a patched low-level backend, with an explicit consumer patch policy, a complete Meta/key schema patch, Codon-owned press/lease/description/viewport/scroll adapters and actual refs instead of the upstream Proxy/HOCs. This would add a new native peer/dev/showcase dependency plus patch-distribution obligations, and still needs the same required native proof. It is not approved or a fallback to adopt silently. A flag-only route or unmodified dependency does not satisfy the demonstrated two-platform contracts. Prefer the source-owned adapter to keep those semantics, source ownership and existing pins under one implementation.

Current construction: the first raw backend adds NativeFocusView, explicit admitted focus/blur requests, full native key records, codegen/autolinking and actual-root ref forwarding including React19 callback cleanup. #### 3.5.22. Native Activation And Release Contract

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-18: “Approve activation/release contract.” Source, public declarations, consumer and matching Release linkage pass at 17b7882; installed acceptance remains OPEN.** Metadata key: `DEC.native-activation.01`. The approved3.5.21 event union has touch and keyboard arms. A further primary-source audit finds that pinned Android ViewGroupClickEvent produces an empty native data map, while iOS RCTViewComponentView exposes a separate accessibilityActivate/onAccessibilityTap route. Neither source establishes a genuine touch-coordinate payload for every native activation. Canonical React Aria3.48 usePress handles virtual activation through semantic start/change, up, end/change and one action; its held-pointer and held-keyboard branches avoid duplicate activation. Physical spoken output remains deferred, but truthful public event typing and native binding remain mandatory3.1.1–3.1.3/3.1.7–3.1.8 capabilities.

Approved public correction, under the existing source-owned backend and without dependencies or SDK flags:

```ts
export const NATIVE_ACTIVATION_SOURCES = ["android-click", "ios-accessibility-activate"] as const
export type TNativeActivationSource = (typeof NATIVE_ACTIVATION_SOURCES)[number]
export type TNativeInteractionEvent = GestureResponderEvent | TNativeKeyboardEvent
export type TNativeActivationData = Readonly<{
  kind: "activation"
  target: number
  source: TNativeActivationSource
  observedAt: number
}>
export type TNativeActivationEvent = NativeSyntheticEvent<TNativeActivationData>
export type TNativePressEvent = TNativeInteractionEvent | TNativeActivationEvent
```

observedAt is the native callback-observation clock, not an invented hardware-input timestamp. Activation has no touch coordinates, key codes or fabricated native down/up. Existing native pointer/key event objects remain unchanged. onPress plus semantic onPressStart/onPressEnd and the canonical onPressUp equivalent admit the truthful three-arm event; raw onPressIn/onPressOut keep the existing touch-or-keyboard interaction type. Callers inspecting kind narrow keyboard versus activation explicitly, then inspect actual touch fields only in the touch arm. Existing source callers, executable declarations and usage examples must migrate coherently rather than casting activation to GestureResponderEvent.

A private codegen/native activation record binds the actual Android click or iOS accessibility-activation callback, with native owner/window/lifetime admission and one-action delivery. Idle activation follows canonical semantic start → change(true) → up → end → change(false) → action using the original activation record; a held pointer completes once and a held keyboard does not gain a duplicate virtual action. Preserve separately forwarded SDK accessibility callbacks and their original events. Raw press-in/out callbacks are not manufactured for a callback that supplied no physical down/up. Real touch/key release supplies semantic onPressUp only for the current admitted, uncancelled cycle. Disabled/pending, replacement/unmount, callback changes and window loss retain3.5.21's guards and actual native default-handling policy. Controlled values, actual refs, palette, geometry and slots remain unchanged.

Verify original native record identity/fields, absence of fictitious coordinates, exact semantic/raw callback order, touch/keyboard preservation, long-press/repeat/re-entry, owner vetoes and all lifetime/guard combinations. Public positive/negative fixtures must prove each arm and press-up contract. Fresh outside-workspace archive/declaration/Metro/native products remain required; actual simulator/emulator activation/default/lifetime cases cannot be replaced with source injection or physical-lane deferral. This decision extends the earlier approved public event API and now has the required owner review under AGENTS.md. The source increment binds Android native click and iOS accessibilityActivate, forwards SDK accessibility callbacks separately and implements semantic release. It is recorded at a separate logical boundary in [3.31.12](experiments.md#33112-native-activation-and-release), retaining every applicable acceptance cell; CP-46 supplies matching fresh consumer/Release linkage at 17b7882, while 110 explicit native activation/release cells and all actual installed proof remain OPEN.

#### 3.5.26. Pagination Capability Contract

This conventional port follows the standing full-goal approval and existing native controls/floating architecture. The audit reads all canonical Pagination source/helper/subcomponents/CSS/labels/icons/index and all 30 root cases, plus the already audited inherited Button/Counter/FormField/NumberInput/Select/ListBox and floating contracts. The unmodified web root suite passes 30/30. A separately executed canonical function produces the compact 131,760-case window oracle (page counts1–60, maxima3–20, both sides/RTL and every valid current page); the native test compares exact serialized outputs without importing web runtime.

Required currentPage/itemsPerPage and their request callbacks remain caller-owned. Numeric counts are finite safe integers, current/page-size are positive, and total items may be zero; explicit invalid zero values reject rather than becoming defaults. Page count is at least one. A current page temporarily beyond the derived count is retained as caller state while only its window placement is bounded; requests are range-guarded, never automatic normalization. The distinct page-input draft initializes once to1 and may be null while empty. Size-change coordination uses the caller's current first-item index, clamps to a valid page (one for an empty result), and requests size before page. Native does not reproduce the canonical stale local page-size mirror: the owner's explicit controlled-value authority takes precedence.

| Capability ID      | Canonical root assertion                                                | Native equivalent / expected outcome                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| CAP.pagination.001 | renders.                                                                | Construct the native root and default primary controls with the actual View ref.                                                                                                                                                |
| CAP.pagination.002 | merges native root className and style without losing computed styles.  | Computed root native styles precede customComponentStyles then final style; registered/array styles, explicit zero and foreground remain meaningful.                                                                            |
| CAP.pagination.003 | maps labeling props onto root aria attributes.                          | Preserve native/ARIA label precedence and ordered same-window ID relationships through the owned host. Native navigation/current semantics and actual inspection remain OPEN until bound/proven.                                |
| CAP.pagination.004 | responds to isDisabled prop.                                            | Global disabled state guards every request and owned descendant; nested props cannot re-enable it.                                                                                                                              |
| CAP.pagination.005 | does not leak wrapper props onto the root element.                      | Consume the complete Pagination API, forwarding only actual native root props.                                                                                                                                                  |
| CAP.pagination.006 | forwards nested PageInput prop objects as component props.              | Forward supported native FormField/NumberInput/Button props and test IDs while protecting owned values, ranges, disabled guards and handlers.                                                                                   |
| CAP.pagination.007 | merges overflow popover and options list styling hooks.                 | Keep popover root/dialog/list and overflow-button style owners independent, with final native override precedence.                                                                                                              |
| CAP.pagination.008 | renders overflow popovers without the raised shell by default.          | Overflow shell defaults raised=false.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| CAP.pagination.009 | allows overflow popovers to opt back into the raised shell.             | Authored raised=true remains effective.                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| CAP.pagination.010 | responds to currentPage prop.                                           | Current page derives from the caller, with no local page mirror or fabricated change on prop replacement.                                                                                                                       |
| CAP.pagination.011 | responds to itemsPerPage prop.                                          | Page size derives from caller itemsPerPage; external changes and repeated owner vetoes stay authoritative.                                                                                                                      |

Compare the complete valid 1..60-page domain against the independent canonical oracle.                                           |
