| CAP.pagination.016 | uses semantic defaults for no-order affordance colors.                  | Use existing semantic native foreground/border roles, including the separator; do not add palette values.                                                                                                                       |
| CAP.pagination.017 | resolves outline order icon colors through registry CSS variables.      | All five orders and fill/outline glyph foreground use existing native theme roles and mounted theme updates.                                                                                                                    |
| CAP.pagination.018 | responds to custom text props.                                          | Preserve every legacy text override; explicit empty text remains authored.                                                                                                                                                      |
| CAP.pagination.019 | supplies grouped labels through its subcomponents.                      | Deep-merge grouped root/primary/counter/input/page-size labels with function labels, native extra captions and existing increment/decrement/close labels.                                                                       |
| CAP.pagination.020 | responds to custom icon props.                                          | Preserve all five icon slots, caller node identity and explicit null suppression; default glyphs use the existing SVG peer.                                                                                                     |
| CAP.pagination.021 | showWithOnePage toggles rendering when numberOfItems <= itemsPerPage.   | One-page visibility follows caller-derived page count and showWithOnePage, including zero items.                                                                                                                                |
| CAP.pagination.022 | supports explicit subcomponent combinations.                            | Preserve the default primary group, empty-array default behavior, ordered/deduplicated chosen groups and separators only between rendered groups.                                                                               |
| CAP.pagination.023 | Preserves the page input draft across parent renders until submission.  | Keep the initially-one/null-capable input draft across parent/theme/value replacements; submit alone requests a bounded page.                                                                                                   |
| CAP.pagination.024 | Uses the latest local page size when preserving the first visible item. | Native uses the current caller page size to preserve the first visible item. The web local-mirror result is intentionally superseded by Gabriel’s explicit standing controlled-value requirement; repeated vetoes cannot drift. |
| CAP.pagination.025 | clicking the current page does not navigate.                            | No page callback when the requested page is already current.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| CAP.pagination.026 | clicking a different page navigates to that page.                       | A different valid page requests exactly that caller update and waits for echo.                                                                                                                                                  |
| CAP.pagination.027 | clicking increment buttons navigates to expected pages.                 | First/previous/next/last requests use current caller values and valid range guards.                                                                                                                                             |

A declined page request does not manufacture an owner echo or force a controlled popup closed.                              |

#### 3.5.50. Toaster Passive Keyboard Observation Plan

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-18 for the private passive observation extension and complete Toaster delivery, with unchanged public APIs/props/payload names/refs/values/pins/palette and required actual native pass-through proof.** Continue feature/native-toaster-completion from clean CP-70 headb173a14. The complete prior Toaster construction/focus contracts remain in force; public availability stays40/53 until the whole family is supportable. Reinspect canonical Toaster/Toast/observer/helpers/types/labels/icons/CSS/index, all38 owned assertions and current native source before delivery. This proposal closes the precise remaining physical-code/non-consuming document-keyboard contract instead of exporting the private shortcut subset.

Actual source oracle fixtures/native-toaster/hotkey.oracle.test.tsx executes20 predicate cases and the real canonical Toaster document handler. The canonical predicate matches KeyboardEvent.code independently of the logical character, treats an empty array as matching every keydown, permits modifier-only/property/method/prototype truthiness, and does not prevent default. Actual repeated matching keydown toggles expansion again. Current native code converts KeyE to logical e, emits no registration for empty/modifier-only arrays, requires nativeDefaultHandled=true at scope dispatch and drops repeats. Existing consuming Escape/arrow/press capture remains correct for its approved purpose; a separate passive path is required.

| Plan item                                           | Concrete implementation boundary and acceptance                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| --------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 3.5.50.1. Private observation protocol              | Add private observed-key registration and a separate observed-key event on the existing CUIKeyboardScope host. Keep existing public NativeKeyboardData/press/focus events, actual refs, component props, payload field names, pins, palette and SDK flags unchanged. Observations do not use the consuming policy selector or its nativeDefaultHandled=true gate. Preserve original native packets and expose no public browser-event facade.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| 3.5.50.2. Platform physical identity                | Derive iOS physical identity from public UIKey.keyCode/HID usage and Android identity from KeyEvent.getKeyCode with explicit SDK-to-code/location tables. Retain raw nativeCode and logical characters independently; never infer physical identity from Unicode. Android documentation identifies getKeyCode as the physical key and warns scan codes vary by device. Unknown/unavailable identity stays explicit Unidentified, with unknown mappings/profile cases still required. Every delivered supported platform code receives an audited table entry and executable vectors.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| 3.5.50.3. Native pass-through                       | Use a passive iOS recognizer in the already owned per-window input architecture: cancelsTouchesInView=false, both delay flags=false, no transition to recognized/began/changed and no prevention/failure relationship that captures another recognizer. Observe real UIPress records, balance repeat/end/cancel lifetimes and retain the existing consuming recognizer unchanged. Android observes before forwarding the original KeyEvent through the unchanged captured-policy/super dispatch path; observation cannot change its return value or suppress default input. No UIWindow method replacement, global swizzle, app delegate/HID hook, new runtime or dependency is approved by this plan.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| 3.5.50.4. Independent leases and multiple listeners | Register actual same-window scope owners with native window/epoch/observation-revision acknowledgement. Snapshot one native event for the live observer leases in registration order, deliver each at most once and recheck each live owner before its callback. One listener's retirement must not discard another still-live listener from the same event. Native event IDs and an independent observation revision distinguish this path from consuming-policy revision; window loss, coverage, background, owner replacement and unmount retire old packets/held state.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| 3.5.50.5. Predicate and host equivalence            | Preserve unrestricted readonly string-array hotkeys: every token checks the mapped source property’s truthiness or exact physical code. Include empty/modifier-only arrays, key/code/location/repeat/composition and standard Event/UIEvent/KeyboardEvent properties and inherited methods. A private evaluator consumes native records; it does not synthesize a DOM KeyboardEvent or a fake editor/ref. DOM target/view identities map to actual native window/target presence; native default-disposition predicates use the already approved synchronous native capture acknowledgement, never asynchronous JS preventDefault. Document exact native host mappings and keep unsupported/ambiguous delivered forms OPEN until resolved, without silently narrowing the public hotkey type.                                                                                              |
| 3.5.50.6. Complete Toaster integration              | Move expansion and focused-toast dismissal onto the passive path, retain source repeat semantics and original current toast/action callbacks, then finish the full public Toaster/toast/constants/labels/types boundary and maintained scene. Preserve actual-focus capture/return, all38 owned assertions, six positions, original typed IDs, queue/action timing, duration/persistent/zero, swipe ownership, independent slots/styles/context and responsive/Reduce Motion behavior. No editor value/selection write, ref proxy, fabricated focus completion or workaround for RN-TEXT-01 is introduced.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| 3.5.50.7. Required proof and stop condition         | First run exact source/mapping/model/lifetime assertions, compile both native adapters against actual locked SDKs and regenerate codegen. Freeze a new public consumer and both matching local Release products after schema changes. A bounded native-input cohort must separately record passive observation plus actual downstream native key/default delivery, repeats, physical identity, multiple listeners, target/window retirement and editor-held values through an existing working authorized transport. Source/mocked/compiled proof cannot pass those installed cells. If the recognizer cannot preserve delivery, stop at that actual failure and return with a concrete alternative plan; do not substitute a window hook, dependency or reduced shortcut API. Existing SystemUI/iOS transport failures remain preserved, with no startup repair/replay or iPhone signing. |

Status: **Approved in full directly by Gabriel on2026-10-22, including exactly one additional combined SDK cohort under3.5.52.9.7.** The explicit reply is “Approve complete §3.5.52.9”. The inspected source path is specific: an original core editor ref.focus reaches the SDK command dispatcher; Scheduler::uiManagerDidDispatchCommand queues its original delegate callback as a rendering update. Off the main queue, UIKit's original RCTMountingManager dispatchCommand enqueues on the main queue. Android's original FabricUIManager dispatchCommand enqueues an original command item; its enqueue method does not execute it. The current field transaction service returns without calling the platform delegate when no real Fabric effects changed, and a rendering callback is not evidence that either platform command executed. FabricUIManager's synchronous property update is not a safe substitute: the pinned SDK explicitly explains why blindly draining pending mounts from an arbitrary stack can delete live views. The synchronous runtime entry uses executeSynchronouslyOnSameThread_CAN_DEADLOCK without a deadline; moving JS onto UI through that API would discard the approved busy-JS bound. No queue pump, private-field reflection, fake/empty transaction, sentinel command, manual text/selection write or ref/delegate/window replacement is an approved repair.

```sh
./gradlew :app:assembleRelease -PreactNativeArchitectures=x86_64 \
  --init-script /absolute/workspace/planning/component-library/fixtures/native-time-picker/sdk-source-build.init.gradle \
  --no-daemon --max-workers=2 --console=plain
```

With shouldCloseOnSelect=false, a date/time picker can hold an internal date-only draft and commit placeholder time before requesting close; a declined value/open request stays declined. Date replacement across the New York daylight-saving boundary changes the offset from−04:00 to−05:00 while retaining13:45:07. This legitimate partial source state must not be removed as if it were an uncontrolled whole-value mirror.

##### 3.5.54.9. Native Pointer-Default Amendment

Status: **Approved directly by Gabriel for the complete pointer-default amendment and all six construction/proof parts below.** The pinned Menu mouse-grace hook suppresses parent pointer interaction and prevents pointer-down defaults while the pointer travels toward an open submenu. The current native window-pointer transport is expressly passive and observes dispatch after capturing its hit path; the owned Android pressable also enables actual native clicks/focus. An asynchronous JavaScript focus/action veto is therefore insufficient evidence for the required native default-prevention contract. This is an inspected source/control-flow discrepancy, not an executed native failure or permission to change the existing observer's default path. Preserve the passive motion transport and all public pointer/keyboard/activation events. The explicit approval covers the separate consuming native input policy under AGENTS.md; independent approved collection-family source construction continues.

| Pointer amendment part                        | Proposed bounded contract                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 3.5.54.9.1. Canonical outcomes                | Exercise the actual pinned Menu in a real browser before admitting a native policy: travel over a sibling toward either-side submenu, two invalid movements, stationary timeout/handoff, pointer down during grace, leaving/reentering, keyboard and genuine virtual activation, nested menus and underlying controls. Retain actual hit targets, default-prevented flags, focus, hover/press/action ordering and first failures. Do not replace browser hit testing with a JSDom style assertion.                                                                                                     |
| 3.5.54.9.2. Private policy boundary           | Add a private scope-owned menu pointer-policy registration alongside the existing scoped key and passive pointer registries. A policy names only its original parent/submenu native targets and current collection/root/surface/window/epoch identities. Native status/observation callbacks report original SDK pointer observations, native hit paths, policy revision and readiness. No caller query, selected values, editor state, new public pointer API, dependency or SDK flag is introduced.                                                                                                  |
| 3.5.54.9.3. Native authority                  | Observe original mouse movement and actual parent/submenu geometry in the same native coordinate space. Apply the complete pinned angle calculation, two-invalid-movement rule,50ms throttle,1000ms inactivity and100ms handoff without guessed frames. Original UI-thread pointer-default suppression must occur before the relevant platform default; a later JavaScript cancel/focus repair cannot substitute. Use the browser outcomes to preserve which events/actions remain allowed, including nested/underlying controls. Do not turn the existing passive observer into a consuming observer. |
| 3.5.54.9.4. Original callbacks and retirement | Keep original native event/ref identities and the full public press/hover lifecycle. A timeout may use a retained original native hit/hover observation for handoff; it must not dispatch a manufactured PointerEvent, fake callback or editor/focus repair. Window loss/replacement, coverage, policy/descriptor retirement, disabled changes and unmount retire pending work and default claims. ##### 3.5.54.10. Menu Ordered Accessibility Mapping Decision

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-21: native linked order/count, with all five original items retained and every proof/guard below unchanged.** The existing all-eight-part3.5.54 approval includes full Menu semantics, but the actual pinned source oracle exposes a representation conflict that the generic approval did not decide. The complete actual-source oracle and its first failures are retained in native-menu-focus/contract.oracle.test.tsx and experiments.md; this is not an inferred native defect.

UIKit's public UIAccessibilityContainer methods expose an ordered element count, elementAtIndex and indexOfElement, and the current native collection backend implements table-specific DataTable/DataTableCell protocols. A Menu must not be disguised as a grid/table, backed by synthesized public events or populated with metadata-only hosts. The pinned SDK header UIAccessibilityContainer.h and Apple's public container documentation distinguish lists from tables. Android's actual CollectionInfo/CollectionItemInfo remains available without changing dependencies or SDK profiles.

- **3.5.54.10.3. Platform implementation.** Android supplies actual CollectionInfo/CollectionItemInfo for a one-dimensional list. Return no fabricated target for offscreen, retired, duplicate, foreign or unacknowledged bindings. - **3.5.54.10.4. Required proof.** Add source declarations and real component cases for flat/section/submenu order, disabled and selected states, filtered/reordered/removed/reinserted items, explicit empty state, real VirtualizedList subsets, RTL and large text. ##### 3.5.54.11. Native Field Semantics And Form Foundation Decision

Status: **Approved in full by Gabriel on2026-10-21, including all eight parts, shared/native APIs and explicit native correspondences below.** The existing all-eight-part3.5.54 approval continues to authorize the current collection/state/family adoption. This amendment makes the previously named TagComboBox form/label obligation concrete: actual core TextInput nodes do not participate in the owned CUIFocusView label/description registry, and the earlier NativeForm proposal in3.5.52.6 names TimePicker as its initial adapter. The private retained-document foundation and scoped iOS SDK prerequisite now pass; the private NativeForm root/programmatic controller, inner ComboBox field binding and NativeFormAction/native admission protocol are implemented below. Persistent original-editor semantics, installed action/focus proof, complete family contexts/public delivery and their acceptance remain OPEN; no unsupported field/form API is exported in advance.

The source evidence is the actual pinned ComboBox/Group/Input/Label/Text/FieldError/ComboBoxValue/Form/useComboBox/useFormReset implementation and the maintained tag-combo-parent oracle. It proves original inner versus outer refs, five parent render fields, source input ID/name placement, separate group/trigger labels, key/text/custom form entries, duplicate names, explicit programmatic requests while disabled/read-only, and retained logical focus across a requested host replacement. The first ID collision probe also observes registered source IDs merging differently after rerenders; do not silently assume that literal IDs always follow ordinary prop-spread precedence. Expand the actual form/reset/validation/order oracles before each receiver is adopted rather than substituting a generic form library.

- **3.5.54.11.1. Complete source-facing family APIs.** Finish native equivalents of the full ComboBox input/button/group/popover/list/state/label/text/error/value contexts and receiver/render/ref/default/named/null-slot surfaces, including current callbacks, original objects, complete validation results, existing renderer/layout/scroll and virtual-focus contracts. Preserve standalone ComboBox and existing primitive signatures during the initial adapter. Add family receivers where a scoped contract is needed; do not silently force unsupported props into an unrelated primitive. Public export/availability still requires the whole-family audit, declarations and maintained owner scene. This decision authorizes the shared field/form foundation and the initial TagComboBox adoption; subsequent control adapters require their own complete source audits and do not acquire implicit source or acceptance promotion.
- **3.5.54.11.2. Original-editor native semantic binding.** Add private codegen/autolinked CUIFieldBinding and CUIFormRoot metadata hosts, with the same zero-size/transparent/nonhit/nonfocus/nonaccessibility rules as the approved collection metadata hosts. They carry only opaque owner/descriptor/revision identities, original target/root tags, semantic IDs/refs, flags and acknowledged native document/window/session data; no query, field value, selected-key set or business object enters native metadata. Resolve the actual existing RCT input and its original backed UITextField/UITextView or ReactEditText through current native ownership and public native hierarchy access. Never replace/subclass/proxy a caller editor/ref, patch its focus methods, modify editing/key delegates, or write its text/value/selection/composition/marked range. Read-only Fabric family/root/revision access may use the same pinned renderer APIs as the existing immutable snapshot reader, in a separately owned form reader; no original ShadowNode/Yoga mutation, new private RNIvars/reflection descriptors or renderer patch is authorized.
- **3.5.54.11.3. Exact SDK metadata and virtual focus.** Android augments the current accessibility delegate while forwarding its existing actions, editing behavior and unowned metadata. UIKit augments public label/hint/traits and AXCustomContentProvider metadata on the original backed editor, retaining original getter behavior for unregistered/retired targets. This includes a narrowly gated accessibilityCustomContent getter/conformance adapter on the exact registered editable classes when the pinned class lacks that public protocol; no other runtime getter, class/ref replacement or input handler interception is included. Retain the latest owner metadata across native commits and restore it when a binding retires. Labels/descriptions/error/value-slot wording resolve actual native text/ref content; native-specific role/required/invalid/expanded captions are explicit localizable overrides plus pinned dictionaries where available. Keep the actual intrinsic editor value and native keyboard focus intact. A virtual active option uses its original current materialized native target and native accessibility-focus request/observation; keyboard focus stays on the editor, offscreen targets are not fabricated and an unsuccessful/unavailable native request is not completion. Apply the approved linked ordered-list count/ordinal correspondence to the TagComboBox listbox, including sections, preserving raw source node.index/state and retaining any literal source ARIA discrepancies in evidence. UIKit lists remain lists, not table proxies. Actual native getters, focus observations and original targets must prove every correspondence; physical spoken-output evidence remains separate.
- **3.5.54.11.4. Actual committed form document and order.** Treat one current Fabric ShadowTree/root as a native form document, including its retained logical Modal/portal/Activity descendants, rather than treating each physical UIWindow as a different form document. CUIFormRoot/CUIFieldBinding capture immutable current family/root/revision and committed preorder for form roots and field descriptors. Use this actual retained order for fields inside a form and explicit form-ID associations outside it, including duplicate names, wrappers, reorder, hidden mounted fields and root replacement. Registration/effect/Map order, coordinates, a visible-row subset and a user-supplied ordering array are not substitutes. Different native form documents do not share an implicit association. Require current complete receipts and original form/field identities; stale, partial, foreign, duplicate or discarded-render registrations cannot submit or reset. Prototype this actual SDK ordering, including Modal/Activity/external fields, before relying on it; if the chosen pinned renderer cannot establish the complete order, return with that evidence rather than falling back to guessed order or dropping those cases.
- **3.5.54.11.5. Concrete NativeForm API.** Add NativeForm with its actual View ref, id, validationErrors, validationBehavior and onSubmit/onReset request callbacks. submitNativeForm(actualRef) and resetNativeForm(actualRef) are explicitly programmatic operations; a small NativeFormAction button adapter supplies submit/reset actions from an original admitted native press/key/activation callback and preserves caller event/ref identity and veto order. Request records expose the actual form ref, ordered readonly string-entry pairs, native-versus-programmatic origin, the original sourceEvent only for the owned native path, and cancellation state/preventDefault for this request. They fabricate no DOM/native event and never perform action/method navigation or a network request. Methods return explicit submitted/reset/invalid/cancelled/retired outcomes; missing readiness or invalid ownership cannot become an empty successful form. Keep unrelated primitive automatic form behavior unchanged and its inherited gates open until its adapter is audited.
- **3.5.54.11.6. Serialization, validation, reset and identity.** The initial TagComboBox adapter serializes the inner ComboBox state, not selected tag objects: source formValue=key/text, null/zero/empty values, allowsCustomValue forcing text, exact names/form association, duplicate ordered entries, disabled exclusion and readonly inclusion. Values come from the current committed authoritative JavaScript field state; native metadata never copies them and a UI/editor read is not an owner echo. Match the actual source native-versus-aria validation policy, all11 validity flags, required/custom/server errors, validation commit/reset timing, default/controlled reset requests and callback order. Invalid native submission requests focus on the actual current first invalid field and records request versus observed completion separately; hidden/inactive/offscreen inability is retained, not faked. Native field IDs retain the existing explicit native-ID/caller precedence and original refs; retain generated/context source IDs as live semantic aliases for native reference resolution rather than reproducing DOM-wide mutable ID rewriting or changing a caller's nativeID behind its back. Source ID-collision outcomes and this explicit native correspondence stay recorded. No alias can resolve a stale/foreign/ambiguous target.
- **3.5.54.11.7. Native admission and cleanup.** Use weak original native view identities, current metadata descriptor/root/document/window/surface/epoch/revision and native sequence acknowledgments. Metadata changes, callback-only updates, guarded state transitions, Activity hiding, disposal, host/editor/form replacement and window changes preserve or retire authority deliberately. Any temporary accessibility override/delegate/getter contribution must fall through/restore without changing the current React-owned editor props or input events. Preserve original public keyboard/press/focus callbacks and balanced lifetimes. Explicit state API requests remain distinct from UI admission and owner echo; metadata inspection, desired focus and accepted commands are never completed UI. No existing Card-only private-field approval, startup retry, guest/service repair, signing or deferred physical gate is borrowed.
- **3.5.54.11.8. Required proof, delivery and stops.** Add paired actual source/native state/form/order/reset/validation traces, default/named/null contexts, real refs/events, callback replacement/unmount, duplicate/zero/empty/string keys and IDs, explicit owner veto/echo, concurrent/aborted/Activity/Modal/external association and stale/foreign/partial/duplicate rejection cases. Compile both exact locked SDK/codegen lanes; inspect actual original input SDK metadata, native hierarchy/order and accessor restoration. Maintain an owner-driven TagComboBox/Form scene with an independent editor and actual VirtualizedList materialization/RTL/large text. At a coherent whole-family integration boundary, freeze fresh archive/declarations/single-React DOM-free graphs, native integration and both matching Release products, then execute only ordinarily authorized installed cohorts through working transport. Retain all first failures and original caps; no assertion relaxation, source/API reduction, editor workaround, dependency/version/palette/tooling/SDK/profile/CI/publication change or startup replay is included. All53 catalogue IDs/477 cells and current43/53 availability remain until the family source boundary is actually delivered; every original native/profile/closure gate remains mandatory.

##### 3.5.54.12. Original Editor Keyboard Defaults Decision

Status: **Approved in full by Gabriel on2026-10-22, including all eight parts, the bounded prototype, shared private phase/default/ack APIs and complete adoption only after positive proof.** The existing3.5.54.11 full-family approval remains effective. Actual source proves autofocus on the original editor, down/up callback delivery, open-before-down callback order, down-before-edit-change/up order and arbitrary key-down preventDefault suppressing the original editing default. Two mutable source probes pass at /private/tmp/codon-native-combo-input-events-source-survey-02-result.json, with their original trace at -input-events-survey-observation.json. Current native CUIKeyboardScope/CUIInputWindow observation is passive and down-only; it cannot advertise a synchronous editing veto from an asynchronous callback. Root keyboardDelegate/layoutDelegate are not missing public source props: they are low-level hook options, as recorded in3.5.54.11. No source keyboard capability is excluded or relabelled as implemented by this proposal.

- **3.5.54.12.1. Exact callback/phase surface.** Add the missing original-editor autofocus and full down/up/cancel callback surface within the approved family receiver. Extend the existing observed-key transport with explicit phases and keep current passive consumers down-only by default. Preserve original target/current original ref, key/code/native code, location, modifiers, composition, repeat, timestamp and monotonic native event identity. No reconstructed press, cloned callback event, public event-method patch or duplicate key stream is permitted. Callback-only changes preserve the current binding; editor/window/Activity/descriptor replacement retires it.
- **3.5.54.12.2. Separate synchronous default owner.** Prototype a private exact-original-editor keyboard-default registration alongside the passive observer, using the pinned Fabric synchronous event facilities and a small private blocking acknowledgement module. Android decides within the existing original dispatchKeyEvent call before its single super dispatch. UIKit must prove public press/gesture arbitration on the exact original editor before its default, with a separate owned recognizer rather than changing the passive observer. The original JavaScript event's preventDefault/isDefaultPrevented result is acknowledged while that exact native dispatch is still live; source internal ComboBox behavior runs before the caller callback in the observed source order. Existing built-in navigation joins this owner so it cannot suppress or execute the same default twice.
- **3.5.54.12.3. Preserve the original editor and default.** A declined veto or no callback lets the original SDK event continue once. An admitted veto prevents that exact original default. No event replay, synthetic key, input replacement/subclass/proxy, input/editor/key delegate patch, text/value/selection/composition write, method swizzle, application-window replacement/interposition, new private getter/field, SDK flag, dependency/pin, signing or service change is approved. If UIKit's public arbitration cannot meet these constraints, the prototype fails and dependent adoption stops with that evidence; no observational callback or declarative approximation silently substitutes for the source veto capability.
- **3.5.54.12.4. Admission and settlement.** The acknowledgement must match one current owner, original editor identity, descriptor/binding, native document/window/epoch, event ID and phase on its original dispatch stack. Stale/foreign/duplicate/late acknowledgements cannot veto a later event. Missing/failed acknowledgements preserve the original platform default once and report a non-success disposition. Exceptions and nested/reentrant requests settle once without leaving a window or editor captured. No persisted promise, held SDK event or later callback can acquire default authority. Bound native retention to the dispatch lifetime and retire every registration on unmount/inactivity/replacement.
- **3.5.54.12.5. Prototype before broad adoption.** First compile both unchanged pinned Android/UIKit SDK/codegen lanes and prove the synchronous acknowledgement/order/lifetime calculations in focused models. Then build fresh source-matched prototype products and use one ordinarily authorized iOS Simulator cohort on the same existing SDK/guest/profile, at most one boot/install/launch and one native test invocation. Use a20-minute total cap with60-second cleanup reserve and existing180/120/90-second boot/install/launch caps. Preserve the first failure and stop; no startup replay, transport/service/guest repair, borrowed Card exception or Android boot/retry is included. Android installed proof remains open under its existing SystemUI obstruction.
- **3.5.54.12.6. Required actual SDK verdicts.** Original hardware-key delivery must show allowed editing once, vetoed editing absent, retained original text/selection/composition and original ref/focus, down/up/repeat/cancel ordering, source open/default/caller order, modifiers, unhandled keys, callback replacement, unmount, Activity/window retirement, stale/foreign/duplicate rejection and accessor/handler cleanup. Record callback request versus SDK editing observation separately. A model, successful compiler, desired focus, source callback, accepted acknowledgement or elapsed timeout is not a native verdict. Native input under busy JS and reentrancy must return without deadlock or an unexplained captured default.
- **3.5.54.12.7. Complete adoption after positive proof.** If the prototype satisfies all constraints, implement the complete family input/context/keyboard/default behavior and its original callback/ref surface under this one approval, preserving standalone primitives until their audited adapter. Promote exact source observations into the existing canonical oracle and maintain the owner Form/TagComboBox/actual VirtualizedList/RTL/large-text/native Intl.ListFormat scene. This approval would cover the shared private phase/default/ack APIs and complete initial TagComboBox adoption rather than separate requests for each routine field or callback.
- **3.5.54.12.8. Source delivery and scope.** Whole-family declarations/exports, fresh frozen outside consumer, single-React DOM-free graphs, native integration and matching Release products remain required. Actual native/profile/physical/closure gates remain separate and intact. Keep all53 stable catalogue IDs/477 dispositions and current43/53 source availability until the genuine public boundary; this proposal does not approve an API reduction, acceptance waiver, publication, merge or extra hosted CI. Continue independent already approved autofocus/form/owner-scene work while this decision is pending.

#### 3.5.60. Native Atomic Keyframe Measurement Decision

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on2026-10-19: “Approve native measurement plan.” All six parts and their feasibility/native acceptance requirements are retained. Private actual-view Fabric input transport and owned build integration are implemented. The scratch intrinsic factory now has source/SDK/library proof; isolated Android services, live measurement integration and actual native admission remain open.** Metadata key: DEC.native-motion-measurement.01. The complete nine-part3.5.56 foundation remains approved and required. This proposal addresses its3.5.56.9 stop condition for an incompatible measurement/tooling need; it does not ask for that complete foundation again. AGENTS.md requires a plan before build-tooling changes and owner review of new architecture. Existing Android is Java-only and the podspec includes ios h/mm sources; adding a shared native measurement evaluator needs explicit CMake/JNI/Gradle/pod source integration. No dependencies, versions, SDK flags, public input payloads, component props, registry or theme contracts change.

The concrete failure is temporal: React probes and their restoration cross native commits, so ordinary geometry acknowledgment cannot prove the temporary endpoint never paints. The current private staging controller deliberately rejects without an actual atomic provider. A model boolean, onLayout, JS microtask, fabricated mount event or final equal rectangle cannot establish native isolation. Ordinary ShadowNode clone/layout is also unsafe: the family event emitter is not replaceable through ShadowNodeFragment, ParagraphShadowNode.layout can update state and emit onTextLayout, and even its const measureContent path can mutate measured-layout caches. The locked source hashes and compiling declaration fixture in native-card/measurement-source.json preserve these findings without claiming runtime isolation.

#### 3.5.61. Isolated Android Intrinsic Services Decision

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on2026-10-19: “Approve isolated Android services.” All four parts and actual native fidelity requirements remain required. Private Android cached-editor/progress services and their factory bridge are implemented; named-font/cache/custom-callback fidelity, live admission and actual native acceptance remain open.** Metadata key: DEC.native-intrinsic-isolation.01. Existing3.5.56/.60 approvals and the full53-ID goal remain active. This is the concrete unsafe-callback stop condition in3.5.60.2, which requires a revised plan rather than calling a shared mutable service. It does not reopen the complete Card foundation or permit an incomplete Card export.

The locked Android TextLayoutManager cached-ID path retrieves the editor's original Spannable and its ReactTextPaintHolderSpan.textPaint. Its font-fitting path writes paint.textSize and replaces/removes font-size spans on the supplied text. Reusing that callback can therefore mutate native editor-owned measurement data. The Android progress manager separately stores measurements in a shared WeakHashMap. The private C++ factory deliberately requires isolated callbacks for both paths; it has no unsafe original-service fallback. Its copied text/paragraph/attachment and independent Yoga paths remain source construction, with actual native fidelity still required.

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-20 for the four cache/asset descriptors and the separately approved two raw style/weight descriptors, corresponding release-retention rules, request-owned resolution and required native proof.** This extends the approved3.5.61 isolated cached-editor service, whose part2 authorizes private reflection only for “that version's helper/result getter.” The existing complete Card scope stays approved; this request concerns additional peer-private cache access. Its public getTypeface creates missing family/style cache entries, while setTypeface can install caller-selected faces that an empty replacement manager would miss. CustomStyleSpan's public family/feature getters plus its private assetManager/privateStyle/privateWeight determine exact font selection; substituting the current surface asset manager could change an uncached span's face. Calling the original span during scratch measurement can reach that shared manager. Capture exact immutable font handles before evaluation, then perform every scratch lookup in a request-owned instance of the same pinned ReactFontManager.

Approve only these four additional read-only descriptors: ReactFontManager.fontCache:Map; ReactFontManager.customTypefaceCache:Map; ReactFontManager$AssetFontFamily.typefaceSparseArray:SparseArray; CustomStyleSpan.assetManager:AssetManager. Resolve/cache descriptor metadata for the locked peer with exact declaring/field types; missing/incompatible access rejects. On the actual native UI thread, copy the needed family/style/custom entries and retain immutable Typeface handles plus the original span asset-manager identity. Never set a field, swap the global manager, mutate an original map/array, call its populating lookup or keep mutable peer containers as evaluator inputs. Preserve all private-field restrictions outside this exact font snapshot exception. Add only the corresponding consumer retention rules; no general reflection surface or new public API is included.

Seed a new request-owned ReactFontManager using its public addCustomFont/setTypeface methods and captured handles. Its actual pinned public getTypeface then preserves custom-font precedence, explicit registered style overrides, exact nearest-style/weight behavior on both sides of API28 and the original asset/system fallback for cache misses, with writes confined to the owned cache. ### 3.6. LinearProgress And Meter Contract

Status: **Approved by Gabriel on 2026-10-17 for paired implementation, tests, showcase, documentation and logical commits.** The audit covers both complete component/helper/CSS/root-suite/index files and the pinned React Aria ProgressBar/Meter render props, range clamping and formatting behavior. These display-only components need no text editor, overlay, collection, new runtime or new theme role. Counter is excluded because it additionally depends on CircularProgress.

| Surface                               | Approved native contract / verification                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Ownership and range                   | Required `value: number`; optional `minValue`/`maxValue` default to 0/100. Values remain caller-owned, including zero and negatives; no local/default value or change callback. Require finite values/bounds and `maxValue > minValue`, clamp presentation to bounds and calculate percentage from the entire range. Reject invalid ranges explicitly rather than emit NaN geometry.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Progress versus meter                 | `LinearProgress` describes task progress and alone accepts `isIndeterminate`. `Meter` describes a measurement and has no indeterminate state. Both expose a real native View ref. Root roles/value semantics are component-owned; native `role="meter"` on iOS and progressbar compatibility on Android are the meter equivalents, subject to later installed/screen-reader acceptance.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Accessible name and value             | Require nonempty `accessibilityLabel` or `aria-label`, with the existing native spelling taking precedence. Use native accessibility range/value text, not DOM label associations. Preserve `valueLabel` as an optional caller-authored string (including empty); otherwise format with existing Intl support, `formatOptions` and an explicit optional `locale`. Default formatting is percentage; non-percent formatting uses the bounded numeric value. Formatting/Intl remains host-owned with no bootstrap or dependency change. Indeterminate progress omits numeric value/text. |
| Dimensions, orientation and direction | Root defaults to 150 × 10 logical units horizontally or 10 × 150 vertically, with a filling track. Retain `height`/`width` as native dimensions, `orientation` horizontal/vertical and `direction` left/right/up/down. Horizontal defaults right; vertical defaults up; directions on the other axis have no effect, matching web behavior. Test zero, percentage dimensions, rerenders and direction/orientation changes without mirrored value state.                                                                                                                                |
| Colors, geometry and elevation        | Retain `barColor`, `trackColor`, `order`, independent `barGeometry`/`trackGeometry` and `raised`. Use existing native ColorValue validation, semantic selected/background/border roles, action backgrounds, radius tokens and established native shadow/elevation. Custom colors override order. No palette or theme-contract edits.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| Customization                         | Retain separate `customStyles`, `customTrackStyles` and `customBarStyles` native style slots. Root precedence remains computed → customStyles → final `style`; a style callback receives percentage/value text (plus indeterminate state only on progress) and the post-custom `defaultStyle`. Track/bar styles override their computed presentation. Preserve native identity/layout props and test that wrapper props do not leak to host views.                                                                                                                                     |
| Motion and unknown progress           | Use the existing core RN Animated capability for bounded 250 ms determinate transitions, respecting Reduce Motion and stopping obsolete/unmounted animations. The numeric accessibility value and callback state reflect current controlled props immediately. Indeterminate progress uses a decorative core ActivityIndicator with no invented numeric progress; it is not exposed as a second accessibility target. No Motion/React Aria/native animation dependency is added.                                                                                                       |
| Explicit native equivalents           | DOM class names, browser refs/events/form associations, React Aria slot context and arbitrary ReactNode value labels are not native APIs. These are documented substitutions, not silent parity claims. These wrappers own their track/bar children as the canonical web wrappers do. Keep native accessibility speech, measured layout, motion and platform rendering as outstanding installed/device evidence.                                                                                                                                                                       |

The complete root web suites each contain 13 cases covering rendering, semantic default color, dimensions, explicit colors, three geometries, five orders/numbered palette roles, raised state, direction, orientation, three style slots, final override precedence and wrapper prop consumption. Map every assertion to native behavior; add inherited-contract tests for bounded ranges/negative and zero values, formatting/value labels, progress-only indeterminacy, accessible names/owned roles, refs/native forwarding, theme changes, callback defaultStyle, animation cleanup and reduced motion. Type tests accept native dimensions/styles/refs and reject unsupported ownership/DOM/indeterminate-meter surfaces. Use focused Jest suites, native contract typecheck/build and showcase composition tests; record installed iOS/Android/physical proof as pending instead of certifying it through mocks.

<a id="ownership-and-accepted-contracts"></a>

## 4. Ownership And Accepted Contracts

`packages/react` owns canonical React source, theme CSS and registry definitions; `packages/react-native` owns shared TypeScript/TSX native components, typed native theme and the approved3.5.21 source-owned Objective-C++/Java interaction adapter. `apps/native-showcase` owns its generic Expo consumer and app-only Intl bootstrap. `apps/web` does not own canonical registry source or artifact generation. `packages/CLI` owns current source-distribution CLI behavior; `apps/docs` owns durable documentation. Sibling repositories remain separate; this work does not edit Wavemap or Waveguide.

Controlled values remain authoritative, including false, zero, empty string/array and contract-defined null. Undefined alone selects the documented uncontrolled/default path. Preserve event-count protection, existing callback/veto semantics, native refs and style precedence. Do not add effect-driven mirrors, uncontrolled fallback or event suppression to make an automated text test pass. Component [types](../../packages/react-native/src/index.ts), focused suites and the curated [native package reference](../../apps/docs/src/content/docs/packages/react-native.md) own the implemented API; past proposal text does not authorize additional APIs.

| Boundary                  | Accepted decision and remaining limit                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| Theme and style           | Keep the hand-authored palette, semantic roles, user scaling, computed → customStyles → final style precedence and owned text context. No NativeWind or new styling runtime. Colocated typed calibration is preferred when a substantive edit benefits; no broad cleanup is authorized.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| Text/Button               | Native targets, refs, labels, scaling, interaction guards and foreground ownership replace DOM/CSS mechanics; closer owner visual and physical evidence remain separate.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Basic controls            | Controlled/default ownership and native disabled/read-only/pending guards remain at activation. Input delegates controlled native synchronization to RN.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| Select/ListBoxItem        | One typed key/null, independent open ownership, caller filtering/collections and safe-area full-screen presentation; missing selection retention is accepted. The bounded 32-attempt positioning algorithm requires actual measurement progress and full-row visibility.                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| TextArea/Radio/RadioGroup | Preserve native multiline/submit/name behavior and required group ownership, nullable selection and same-value suppression.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| NumberInput               | Keep raw draft separate from nullable committed numeric value, bounded decimal model, final native-event publication, bounds/precision/step guards and explicit child prop ownership. App consumers prepare Intl before native barrel import.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| Modal/AlertDialog         | Caller-controlled ownership, one host/leaf chain, context/draft retention, safe-area/keyboard/window geometry and callback-before-close semantics; public nesting and broader compositions retain their acceptance gates.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| PlaceholderText/ComboBox  | Single typed selection key/null, independent value/query/open axes, caller-filtered readonly async items, guarded rows, owned refs and distinct root/input/button/group/list slots. Default full-screen presentation is accepted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| Deferred web capabilities | Anchored/inline placement, alternate triggers, default collections/filtering/collation, committed custom free text, multiple selection, DOM form/class/portal behavior and web focus wrapping need explicit native equivalents or a documented exclusion before parity certification. No capability is silently removed from the catalogue.                                                                                                                  |
| React source distribution | Keep manifest/graph/CSS ownership, advisory CLI findings, install/update/diff/remove safety and existing proof packets. Curated [registry contracts](../../apps/docs/src/content/docs/registry/contracts.md), [ingest](../../apps/docs/src/content/docs/registry/ingest.md) and [CLI lifecycle](../../apps/docs/src/content/docs/cli/consumer-lifecycle.md) own settled behavior. SourceRef strings in immutable proof packets remain historical provenance. |
