<a id="verification-gaps"></a>

## 7. Verification Gaps

Canonical web React component contracts are the minimum native verification baseline. Verify public behavior, callbacks, refs, labels, dimensions/geometry/size, focus/validation, named slots and override precedence, then add native keyboard/window/accessibility proof. Test counts, mocks and a passing repeated native typing case cannot certify coverage parity.

| Canonical web assertion                                         | Current native evidence                                                                                                                             | Remaining verification / disposition                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Placeholder b11/italic/placeholder color/default alignment      | Direct native leaf assertions, provider overrides, scaling and Text inheritance boundary                                                            | Source tier covered; installed family acceptance incomplete.                                                                        |
| Placeholder center alignment, element type, custom class/styles | Center alignment and native style precedence tested; native ref/labels/events tested                                                                | DOM classes/elements follow the accepted native Text equivalent/exception; no DOM emulation.                                        |
| ComboBox renders                                                | Source outcome cases render trigger/query/list/Close                                                                                                | First installed typing gate failed; receipt retains it.                                                                             |
| Height and width props                                          | Direct iOS/Android default minima, authored height/width, percentage/auto dimensions and computed/custom/final dimension precedence                 | Source tier covered; installed geometry and touch-target acceptance remain separate.                                                |
| Color prop                                                      | Isolated foreground propagation to summary/query/default glyph; button foreground override remains local to owned summary/icon                      | Source tier covered; installed color/visual review remains separate.                                                                |
| Rounded/round/orthogonal geometry                               | All three radii asserted on summary/query groups, trigger and query input                                                                           | Source tier covered; installed geometry remains separate.                                                                           |
| Small/medium/large text size                                    | All three summary/query sizes asserted, with provider font-size changes on the mounted component                                                    | Source tier covered; native large-text layout/scaling acceptance remains separate.                                                  |
| enableFocusStyle and offsetFocusRing                            | Trigger/query focus and blur drive actual group styles; style opt-out and zero/default offsets asserted                                             | Source tier covered with simulated events; native focus/keyboard behavior remains separate.                                         |
| Disabled                                                        | Repeated opening/editing/result guards and controlled takeover tested                                                                               | Installed disabled behavior remains pending for this family.                                                                        |
| isOpen                                                          | Three authoritative axes, repeated vetoes and programmatic owner transitions tested                                                                 | Installed owner/open/action matrix remains pending.                                                                                 |
| Error, warning, success borders and error priority              | Isolated states, warning-over-success/error priority and removal back to baseline asserted on both groups                                           | Source tier covered; installed visual states remain separate.                                                                       |
| Root, input, group and button style slots                       | Each root/input/button/group/list slot independently changes rendered output; summary-button foreground and existing final root precedence asserted | Source tier covered; no additional slots or styling API introduced.                                                                 |
| Default/custom icon                                             | Default chevron geometry and open/closed rotation, authored replacement and explicit null override asserted                                         | Source tier covered through decorative host-node inspection; pixel/physical review remains separate.                                |
| className/style retain computed root styles                     | Native customStyles/final style callback precedence and post-custom defaultStyle tested                                                             | Class plumbing is an explicit native exception; preserve equivalent native style precedence tests.                                  |
| Wrapper props do not leak to root                               | Consumed wrapper props absent on native root; native identity/layout/pointer/collapsable props forward directly                                     | Source tier covered; unsupported public surfaces remain rejected by existing types.                                                 |
| Custom internal labels and empty copy                           | Separate native trigger/query/Close labels, aliases, invalid-name guards and custom empty text tested                                               | Names deliberately match native targets rather than a DOM group/Popover; installed speech/localization acceptance remains separate. |

The focused [ComboBox suite](../../packages/react-native/src/components/ComboBox/__tests__/ComboBox.test.tsx) now passes 55 cases, including 24 added customization cases. This closes the listed source-assertion debt without production changes. Rendered host styles/props, simulated focus events and mocked platform defaults do not prove actual layout, keyboard delivery or physical accessibility. [The evidence companion](experiments.md#5-combobox-customization-coverage) records the exact verification tier.

Full ComboBox installed acceptance still needs first result activation after focused typing, keyboard-active Close/Back, nested context/draft/theme, disabled/owner-veto behavior, large text/rotation/removal and both platform gates. TagComboBox/Table/date leaves are not promoted by diagnostic success. Physical speech/localization/touch/ARM evidence remains distinct.

<a id="rn-text-01-bounded-investigation"></a>

## 8. RN-TEXT-01 Bounded Investigation

Gabriel authorizes this run on 2026-10-16 with logical commits and preserved controlled values, pins and palette. Compare the prior first raw N-only failure against its passing warm repeat before any production repair. First inspect the complete event timelines and pinned RN JS/Fabric/UITextField adapter source; key callbacks are proposal-path observations, not proof of applied text.

The frozen comparison permits at most two new raw attempts after real callback validation: first after guest boot and one warm application restart. Keep the frozen Release application, one unpaced Native query text call, initially empty controlled query and keyboard/correction defaults. A private inspector variant may pause only after attaching its immutable focused verdict so a bounded read-only LLDB snapshot can compare UIKit marked/selected text and RN/delegate state. Label observer timing explicitly; no breakpoint, setter, swizzle, native production change or typing intervention is permitted during the burst. A post-verdict snapshot cannot reconstruct the full mutation sequence.

The continuation authorized on 2026-10-16 repairs and validates XCTest transport in one run capped at 60 minutes, with at most three repair preflights, one per-user simulator-service recycle while all guests are stopped and one fresh guest on the existing runtime if needed. Replay frozen products directly through their xctestrun, keep installer/PID observations optional and require the actual Transport 1 callback attachment, not merely process launch or a passing controller. Validate the selected observer warm and after guest shutdown/reboot before the two raw cases. Keep native-session progress, fixed silence/total caps, failed assertions and incomplete results. A successful repair is a logical commit boundary before comparison findings.

The preceding source/trace investigation completed without a native extension: two preflights failed to reach methods after one retained-guest recovery, yielding zero new raw attempts or snapshots. Preserve that outcome in [the existing experiment section](experiments.md#9-current-raw-firstwarm-investigation); it does not invalidate the earlier successful transport or failed/passing raw traces. Do not erase guests, reset security/keyboard preferences, expand case order/correction/pacing, rerun Input/ComboBox, change production synchronization or dependencies, publish upstream, or sign for iPhone. Restore/stop task guests and retain partial results if this continuation reaches its bound.

The continuation completes its native budget with one service recycle, one fresh same-runtime guest and three repair preflights. Three callback passes in the retained guest are followed by a raw controller startup regression before any method or typing; they do not prove general transport stability. The final fresh-guest configuration passes callback validation first/warm/reboot, then immediately completes exactly two raw methods. First raw shows full native-observed text while controlled channels remain Na; warm raw agrees on the corrupted Na texttive query. Both remain focused with keyboard active and fail the intended phrase. Neither bounded post-verdict LLDB attempt returns native state. The private scheme does not remove Xcode's internal debugger-wait flag, and no unique recovery cause is claimed. See [the comparison and limits](experiments.md#9-current-raw-firstwarm-investigation). Stop here with RN-TEXT-01 open; native change-publication, controlled-write and selection/composition evidence is still required before a production remedy. All task guests are stopped, frozen identities and prior seals are verified, and signing remains deferred.

The subsequent LLDB/native-trace continuation is authorized on 2026-10-16, capped at 60 minutes with at most two task-owned host-probe attachments, an initial three simulator attachments and two instrumented raw methods at most. After automatic approval review rejects an over-budget replay, Gabriel explicitly approves one additional capped attachment/harness replay to correct the agent-authored expression compiler error after successful attach/import; no earlier typing ran and the original 60-minute deadline stays fixed. Keep the same retained guest, frozen app/source/pins/palette and deferred iPhone signing; no security reset, guest erase or production repair is included. Validate real attach/register-memory read/detach/resume before tracing. The current native-tracing request permits bounded auto-continue breakpoints in a separately labeled observer condition, superseding the preceding frozen comparison's prohibition for this instrumented phase only. A private XCTest gate may wait before the unchanged unpaced typing call; record scheduling effects and preserve original focused assertions. The debugger may read native event counters, flags, proposal arguments and controlled-write arguments, but may not set values, patch guard flags or change keyboard defaults. Keep attach/symbol-load/evaluation failures distinct. Commit validated session support and then the trace findings at logical boundaries; keep evidence in the existing experiment section.

The LLDB continuation stops with real simulator attach/register-memory read/detach/resume proof and one corrected private preflight callback pass, but without a live native mutation trace. Attachment03 reaches UIKit import and then fails on the agent's metadata-expression compilation; its corrected wrapper passes offline syntax checks. The single approved final replay fails during application launch before the pretyping gate, so it consumes no fourth attachment and performs no typing. Actual totals are three simulator attachments, three gated raw invocations and zero native breakpoint events or focused text verdicts. [The evidence companion](experiments.md#10-lldb-attachment-and-native-trace) preserves these distinct outcomes. The native run ends within its original bound, all task guests stop and frozen identities remain unchanged. RN-TEXT-01 and the frozen comparison remain open. The next prerequisite is dependable no-editor startup plus a minimal successful live metadata read; the unvalidated larger probe must not trigger another implicit replay or production workaround.

The next startup/minimal-metadata continuation is authorized on 2026-10-16 with logical commits and a 45-minute bound: at most three no-editor startup validations, one retained-guest reboot, two simulator debugger attachments and one instrumented raw invocation after prerequisites pass. A private observer may require and activate a host-prelaunched frozen app instead of terminating/launching it through XCTest; callback proof also requires the same owned PID/birth/executable identity before and after the method. Require at least two actual callback passes, including one after reboot, then a minimal live metadata read before tracing. Preserve failed attempts and distinct startup/observer conditions. No new guest, service/security reset, app rebuild, keyboard/default/pacing change, production repair or iPhone signing is included. Commit validated startup support separately from the live-probe/trace findings, then stop and seal within the fixed bound.

The startup/minimal-metadata continuation ends without a validated repair: one capped host-prelaunch attempt times out before XCTest begins, and later process presence does not prove readiness. Actual counts are zero callback validations, zero debugger attachments, zero live metadata probes and zero typing. The final read-only process observation exceeds the stated deadline by about two minutes; record that overrun rather than treating it as an authorized extension. Only cleanup follows. [The experiment record](experiments.md#101-startup-and-minimal-metadata-continuation) retains the failed contrast and unexecuted probe. Preserve controlled ownership and frozen identities; the no-editor startup and minimal live-read prerequisites remain open.

The delayed-launch/host-load continuation is authorized on 2026-10-16 with a controller-enforced 25-minute investigation deadline and five-minute native cleanup reserve. Use the retained guest only, at most two host launch invocations, two no-editor XCTest validations, two debugger attachments and one instrumented raw invocation after prerequisite proof. Compare host CPU/memory/swap before and after boot, and record direct task counters and bounded app/client stack samples during launch. A timed-out client remains a failure; a later uniquely owned app born after that invocation may be considered without relaunch, but only actual callback delivery and the same live PID/UID/birth/executable can validate readiness. Require callback proof and minimal live metadata before tracing. Preserve controlled values, source/app bytes, pins, palette and keyboard defaults; no new guest, service/security reset, app rebuild or iPhone signing is included. The controller must enter cleanup when its fixed deadline is reached, independently of chat polling.

The delayed-launch investigation ends inside its native bound with a 1.29-second app launch and a stable app identity, but XCTest aborts during Accessibility/EventKitUI asset initialization before any selected method. Host paging/swap and CPU pressure are measured; no unique cause for all prior delays is claimed. The synthetic bootstrap error is corrected in method-verdict reporting while preserving failed text assertions and original receipts. [The experiment record](experiments.md#102-delayed-app-launch-and-host-load) retains the exact path, limits and unavailable optional samples. Automatic cleanup stops all task guests after 5.21 minutes of investigation. Actual callback passes, metadata probes and typing remain zero; tracing stays gated on real Accessibility/bootstrap and callback readiness. No production component or controlled-value change follows.

The Accessibility-bootstrap continuation is authorized on 2026-10-16 with a controller-enforced 25-minute investigation cutoff and five-minute native cleanup reserve. Use one retained-guest boot, two explicit first/warm host-launch/callback conditions, one minimal metadata attachment and at most one separately gated trace attachment/raw invocation. A fixed observation of CPU quiet may defer startup for at most 60 seconds; it does not prove test readiness. Keep Accessibility and its watchdog enabled. Preserve a failed first condition and still execute the planned warm condition within the original bound. A passing latest callback permits the minimal live read; tracing requires both callbacks and the metadata read to pass. Preserve controlled values, frozen source/app/inspector products, pins, palette and keyboard defaults; no new guest, service/security reset, app rebuild or iPhone signing is included. Stop native work and automatically clean up at the fixed cutoff, regardless of chat polling.

The Accessibility continuation preserves a cold AX-loaded-notification timeout and a distinct warm in-method portrait-setup failure while querying the system active-app list. Neither reaches callback proof, so metadata and tracing stay gated off. A private observer candidate now activates and locates the owned app before requesting portrait; it compiles in a separate cache but has no native validation and does not claim to fix cold AX bootstrap. [The experiment record](experiments.md#103-accessibility-bootstrap-and-activation-ordering) preserves both outcomes, observed host boot workloads and source/binary identities. All task guests stop within the native bound. The next stop condition is an actual callback pass using the activation-first candidate, then a minimal live metadata read before any trace; controlled values, frozen production bytes, pins, palette and deferred iPhone signing remain intact.

The activation-first validation ends with three preflight conditions and no callback proof: two fail during XCTest AX bootstrap and one is interrupted during runner acquisition. At the owner's request, helper defaults become 600 seconds total/180 seconds silence; an explicit 600/300 replay advances through long startup work, then exits on XCTest's own AX timeout after 199.01 seconds. Neither larger controller limit fires. [The experiment record](experiments.md#104-activation-first-validation-and-timeout-allowances) preserves the changed allowance, retained-guest reboot and unchanged overall deadline. All task guests stop, frozen values/pins/palette remain unchanged and signing stays deferred. The candidate's activation sequence, minimal metadata and tracing remain unvalidated; RN-TEXT-01 remains open.

The next 40-minute startup/acceptance run validates a narrower private-observer repair: replace redundant orientation-setting requests with explicit portrait/device-window preconditions. One actual Transport 1 callback passes with the owned app identity preserved. Cold AX initialization remains fragile. The original frozen ComboBox priority fixture is then resumed after correcting an agent-owned installed-folder assumption; four priority acquisitions fail before the selected method through AX loading or a Busy runner-launch denial, including after private-runner reinstallation. No editor typing or new ComboBox verdict occurs. [The experiment record](experiments.md#105-patient-startup-and-combobox-priority-acceptance) preserves the original deadline, seven distinct XCTest outcomes, both fixes and restoration. Native family acceptance and RN-TEXT-01 remain open; require reliable priority-observer startup before another editor budget. Controlled values, pins, palette and deferred iPhone signing are preserved.

The bounded priority-runner repair ends within its 40-minute investigation/five-minute cleanup allowance with zero callbacks, selected methods or typing. A separate runner identity/cache repeats the AX/Busy failures; a simulator-supported profile omits optional checker injection but ends in a bootstrap kill. Two destination-artifact commands are unsupported simulator configuration rejections; the warm-observation guard now rejects that repetition. [The evidence companion](experiments.md#106-priority-runner-identity-and-replay) retains all five replay commands, internal 120-second control-session timing and the earlier passing contrast without claiming a unique cause. The original ComboBox acceptance remains gated. The next stop is deliberate review of test-manager/AX startup and lab/runtime options before another editor budget; this receipt authorizes no further replay. All task guests stop, app67/source62/pins/palette remain frozen and iPhone signing stays deferred.

The control-session investigation records complete replies in 29 ms and 17 ms after explicitly starting the existing on-demand test-manager job before Xcode. Neither prepared condition hits the earlier native 120-second control timeout. Preflight still reaches no selected method: the first bootstrap kill overlaps private-runner sampling, and a validation without runner probes is denied as Busy before launch. [The experiment record](experiments.md#107-control-session-activation-and-bootstrap) preserves the added retained-guest boot, unchanged original deadline and observer limits. The original ComboBox acceptance remains gated with zero callbacks or typing. Forty focused helper cases and the actual control-log contrasts pass; all task guests stop and frozen source/app/pins/palette remain intact. Further iOS work should target runner launch/test-bundle bootstrap after a healthy control reply. No production text repair or additional replay is inferred, and iPhone signing stays deferred.

<a id="working-and-evidence-rules"></a>

## 9. Working And Evidence Rules

Use `feature/<description>` branches unless Gabriel explicitly chooses another name. Commit one coherent library step at a natural boundary: audited contracts, component source/tests, then maintained consumer examples/reference and evidence. Diagnostic work requires its own explicit scope; the active delivery goal excludes further XCTest startup investigation. Do not generate a new roadmap, pass/receipt pair or duplicate status table per timeout. Keep current work here and experiment details in experiments.md. Raw command logs, captures, source/binary hashes and private harnesses remain in evidence packets; stable explanations graduate into curated docs.

Keep every section heading below the document title in decimal hierarchy (`## 1.`, `### 1.1.`, `#### 1.1.1.`). Use the numbered rubric, scenario and profile references in roadmap prose and matrix labels; stable evidence codes are metadata only. Before each planning commit, verify sequential heading numbers, contents targets, local section links and legacy anchors in both planning files.

Use the narrowest meaningful verification: affected fixture/helper checks, strict native-consumer types where changed, native preflight/raw verdicts, frozen source/archive/installed identities, targeted planning Markdown/link checks, docs build where curated pages change, normal commit hooks and git diff --check. Do not rebuild unchanged production code or rerun broad suites simply to fill a receipt. Preserve local Git identity, hooks, failed native assertions and exact observer revisions. Branch pushes and library pull requests are authorized under §9.1; merge, unrelated report sending and publication remain unauthorized.

Never execute/import helpers inside a SEALED packet or write beneath it. Copy required helpers to a fresh root and rewrite roots before use. Verify inherited manifests as data; retain the two known pre-seal unlisted bytecode files in the oldest packet. New native inspection must be labeled separately from unchanged application identity. Planning migration uses local Git revision db54a8a for complete former documents and preserves provenance strings rather than rewriting historical evidence. See the [migration lookup](experiments.md#12-historical-document-lookup).

### 9.1. Pull Request Cadence

**Continuation and revised goal authorized by Gabriel on2026-10-24:** after the next coherent library PR, create one new local chat in this Codon UI project, transfer important current context and resume goal work there. Do not manufacture a fixture-only PR to trigger the transfer. Carry the exact branch/PR/source identities, current53-ID/477-cell disposition,43/53 availability, immediate TimePicker blocker, sealed DATA ONLY boundaries, unused/consumed approvals, normal commit authority and three-skip/one-full CI cadence. The new chat must establish an active goal using the following revised objective; the current exposed goal tools cannot edit the existing objective or change its blocked status back to active.

Complete the full53-component native construction and acceptance contract against canonical React sourceddc9f066f67af4742e05e04bd870726141c44129. Prioritize completing TimePicker as counterpart44, then complete the remaining components. Match the React components’ complexity and behavior; go beyond it only for a demonstrated native requirement, explained explicitly. Reuse small shared typed modular helpers defined once. Infrastructure changes require a reproduced blocker to the named component being delivered; stop the investigation when that question is answered. Do not let speculative analysis, framework expansion, planning churn, simulator repair, broad verification or supporting-work PRs displace component delivery. Use the narrowest meaningful checks, preserve normal hooks and the authorized CI cadence, and repeat broader checks only when invalidated or expressly required. Report concrete component behavior delivered, source count, actual verification and the precise remaining blocker; supporting code, builds and PRs do not count as component completion. Preserve every accepted API/input/default/ref/appearance/context/form/consumer/installed/profile gate, all477 cells, sealed evidence and explicit owner/physical deferrals. Respect native-attempt limits and existing commit/push/draft-PR authority; no merge, release, publication, dependency or architecture expansion follows without its required authorization. Continue until complete or a specific owner decision is truly required.

**Authorized by Gabriel on 2026-10-18:** open a pull request for the accumulated library work now, allowing one or two closeout passes to make the current increment reviewable. The initial PR is an explicit exception to the size target because the existing feature branch already contains the accumulated construction. Target the repository's actual default branch, develop, and describe source availability, verification tiers, private/incomplete families and unresolved acceptance honestly. Do not wait for all 53 families or installed acceptance to finish before opening this construction PR.

Continue the full goal autonomously through subsequent feature branches and PRs at approximately 2,000 changed lines (additions plus deletions, including tests and documentation), or a substantial coherent swathe of library work. Treat this as a reviewability target rather than a hard quota: stop at a logical feature/contract boundary, identify unusually large generated changes separately and explain a meaningful overrun. Keep coherent commits smaller than the PR and retain normal hooks/local identity. Open the next PR promptly when that boundary is reached rather than accumulating another large branch or waiting for routine approval.

**CI cadence authorized by Gabriel on 2026-10-18:** PR #19 is the baseline. Count only subsequent library-goal PRs, independently of unrelated repository PR numbers: ordinals 1–3 may skip hosted CI, ordinal 4 runs the full existing CI surface, then repeat the three-skip/one-run cycle. Updates/retries on the same PR do not advance the count. Retain local meaningful tests/builds/types and normal Git hooks on every increment; skipped hosted CI never becomes passing acceptance evidence. Record each goal PR's ordinal and CI disposition in the existing experiment companion when opening it.
